 ^1-1

 ^1-3

具壽婆帝利迦， ^1-29

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-33]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-34]]

舍利子， ^1-34

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-35]]

大目乾連； ^1-35

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-36]]

惟阿難陀住於學地。 ^1-36

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-37]]

如是等諸大聲聞， ^1-37

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-38]]

各於晡時 ^1-38

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-39]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-40]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-41]]

頂禮佛足， ^1-41

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-42]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-43]]

退坐一面。 ^1-43

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-44]]

復有菩薩摩訶薩，百千萬億人俱—— ^1-44

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-45]]

有大威德，如大龍王， ^1-45

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-46]]

名稱普聞，眾所知識， ^1-46

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-47]]

施戒清淨，常樂奉持， ^1-47

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-48]]

忍行精勤，經無量劫， ^1-48

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-49]]

超諸靜慮，繫念現前， ^1-49

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-50]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-51]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-55]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-56]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-57]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-58]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-59]]

制諸外道，令起淨心， ^1-59

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-60]]

轉妙法輪， ^1-60

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-67]]

有大堅固力， ^1-67

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-68]]

歷事諸佛， ^1-68

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-69]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-70]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-231]]

願樂欲聞殊勝妙法。 ^1-232

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-237]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-238]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-239]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-240]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-241]]

我當為大眾， ^1-241

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-242]]

宣說如是經； ^1-242

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-243]]

并四方四佛， ^1-243

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-244]]

威神共加護： ^1-244

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-245]]

東方阿閦尊， ^1-245

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-246]]

南方寶相佛， ^1-246

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-247]]

西方無量壽， ^1-247

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-248]]

北方天鼓音。 ^1-248

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-249]]

我復演妙法， ^1-249

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-250]]

吉祥懺中勝， ^1-250

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-251]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-252]]

淨除諸惡業。 ^1-252

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-253]]

及消眾苦患， ^1-253

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-254]]

常與無量樂， ^1-254

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-255]]

一切智根本， ^1-255

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-256]]

諸功德莊嚴。 ^1-256

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-257]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-262]]

眷屬悉分離， ^1-262

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-263]]

彼此共乖違， ^1-263

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-264]]

珍財皆散失。 ^1-264

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-265]]

惡星為變怪，或被邪蠱侵， ^1-265

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-266]]

若復多憂愁，眾苦之所逼， ^1-266

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-267]]

睡眠見惡夢， ^1-267

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-268]]

因此生煩惱。 ^1-268

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-269]]

是人當澡浴， ^1-269

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-270]]

應著鮮潔衣， ^1-270

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-271]]

於此妙經王， 甚深佛所讚， ^1-271

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-272]]

專注心無亂，讀誦聽受持。 ^1-272

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-273]]

由此經威力，能離諸災橫， ^1-273

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-274]]

及餘眾苦難，無不皆除滅。 ^1-274

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-275]]

護世四王眾， ^1-275

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-276]]

及大臣眷屬， ^1-276

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-277]]

無量諸藥叉，一心皆擁衛。 ^1-277

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-278]]

大辯才天女， ^1-278

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-279]]

尼連河水神， ^1-279

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-280]]

訶利底母神， ^1-280

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-281]]

堅牢地神眾， ^1-281

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-282]]

梵王帝釋主， ^1-282

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-283]]

龍王緊那羅， ^1-283

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-284]]

及金翅鳥王， ^1-284

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-285]]

阿蘇羅天眾， ^1-285

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-286]]

如是天神等，并將其眷屬， ^1-286

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-287]]

晝夜常不離。 ^1-287

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-288]]

皆來護是人， ^1-288

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-289]]

我當說是經，甚深佛行處； 諸佛祕密教， ^1-289

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-290]]

千萬劫難逢。 ^1-290

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-291]]

若有聞是經， ^1-291

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-292]]

能為他演說， ^1-292

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-293]]

若心生隨喜， ^1-293

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-294]]

或設於供養； ^1-294

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-295]]

如是諸人等， ^1-295

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-296]]

當於無量劫， ^1-296

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-297]]

常為諸天人，龍神所恭敬。 ^1-297

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-298]]

此福聚無量， ^1-298

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-299]]

數過於恒沙， ^1-299

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-300]]

讀誦是經者， ^1-300

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-301]]

當獲斯功德。 ^1-301

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-302]]

亦為十方尊， ^1-302

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-303]]

深行諸菩薩， ^1-303

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-304]]

擁護持經者， ^1-304

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-305]]

令離諸苦難。 ^1-305

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-306]]

供養是經者， ^1-306

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-307]]

如前澡浴身， ^1-307

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-308]]

飲食及香花， ^1-308

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-309]]

恒起慈悲意。 ^1-309

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-310]]

若欲聽是經， ^1-310

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-311]]

令心淨無垢； ^1-311

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-312]]

常生歡喜念， ^1-312

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-313]]

能長諸功德。 ^1-313

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-314]]

若以尊重心， ^1-314

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-315]]

聽聞是經者； ^1-315

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-316]]

善生於人趣， ^1-316

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-317]]

遠離諸苦難。 ^1-317

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-318]]

彼人善根熟， ^1-318

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-319]]

諸佛之所讚； ^1-319

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-320]]

方得聞是經，及以懺悔法。」 ^1-320

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-321]]

 ^1-321

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-322]]

金光明最勝王經如來壽量品第二_爾時，王舍大城有一菩薩摩訶薩，名曰妙幢，已於過去無量俱胝那庾多百千佛所，承事供養，殖諸善根。 ^1-322

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-323]]

是時妙幢菩薩獨於靜處，作是思惟：「以何因緣，釋迦牟尼如來壽命短促，唯八十年？ ^1-323

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-324]]

」復作是念：「如佛所說，有二因緣，得壽命長。 ^1-324

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-325]]

云何為二？ ^1-325

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-326]]

一者、不害生命，二者、施他飲食。 ^1-326

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-327]]

然釋迦牟尼如來曾於無量百千萬億無數大劫，不害生命，行十善道，常以飲食惠施一切飢餓眾生，乃至己身血肉骨髓，亦持施與，令得飽滿，況餘飲食。 ^1-327

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-328]]

」_時彼菩薩於世尊所，作是念時，以佛威力，其室忽然廣博嚴淨，帝青琉璃種種眾寶，雜彩間飾，如佛淨土，有妙香氣過諸天香，芬馥充滿。 ^1-328

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-329]]

於其四面各有上妙師子之座，四寶所成，以天寶衣而敷其上。 ^1-329

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-330]]

復於此座有妙蓮花，種種珍寶以為嚴飾，量等如來自然顯現。 ^1-330

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-331]]

於蓮花上有四如來，東方不動，南方寶相，西方無量壽，北方天鼓音。 ^1-331

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-332]]

是四如來各於其座加趺而坐，放大光明，周遍照耀王舍大城，及此三千大千世界，乃至十方恒河沙等諸佛國土，雨諸天花，奏諸天樂。 ^1-332

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-333]]

爾時，於此贍部洲中及三千大千世界，所有眾生，以佛威力，受勝妙樂，無有乏少。 ^1-333

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-334]]

若身不具，皆蒙具足，盲者能視，聾者得聞，瘂者能言，愚者得智，若心亂者得本心，若無衣者得衣服，被惡賤者人所敬，有垢穢者身清潔，於此世間所有利益，未曾有事，悉皆顯現。 ^1-334

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-335]]

_爾時，妙幢菩薩見四如來及希有事，歡喜踊躍，合掌一心，瞻仰諸佛殊勝之相，亦復思惟釋迦牟尼如來無量功德，惟於壽命生疑惑心：「云何如來功德無量，壽命短促唯八十年？ ^1-335

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-336]]

」_爾時，四佛告妙幢菩薩言：「善男子！汝今不應思忖如來壽命長短。 ^1-336

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-337]]

何以故？ ^1-337

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-338]]

善男子！我等不見諸天世間梵、魔、沙門、婆羅門等，人及非人，有能算知佛之壽量，知其齊限；惟除無上正遍知者。 ^1-338

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-339]]

」時四如來欲說釋迦牟尼佛所有壽量，以佛威力，欲色界天諸龍、鬼神、健闥婆、阿蘇羅、揭路茶、緊那羅、莫呼洛伽，及無量百千億那庾多菩薩摩訶薩，悉來集會，入妙幢菩薩淨妙室中。 ^1-339

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-340]]

_爾時，四佛於大眾中，欲顯釋迦牟尼如來所有壽量，而說頌曰：_「一切諸海水，可知其渧數；_無有能數知，釋迦之壽量。 ^1-340

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-341]]

_析諸妙高山，如芥可知數；_無有能數知，釋迦之壽量。 ^1-341

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-342]]

_一切大地土，可知其塵數；_無有能數知，釋迦之壽量。 ^1-342

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-343]]

_假使量虛空，可得盡邊際；_無有能度知，釋迦之壽量。 ^1-343

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-344]]

_若人住億劫，盡力常算數；_亦復不能知，世尊之壽量。 ^1-344

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-345]]

_不害眾生命，及施於飲食；_由斯二種因，得壽命長遠。 ^1-345

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-346]]

_是故大覺尊，壽命難知數；_如劫無邊際，壽量亦如是。 ^1-346

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-347]]

_妙幢汝當知，不應起疑惑；_最勝壽無量，莫能知數者。 ^1-347

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-348]]

」_爾時，妙幢菩薩聞四如來說釋迦牟尼佛壽量無限，白言：「世尊！云何如來示現如是短促壽量？ ^1-348

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-349]]

」_時四世尊告妙幢菩薩言：「善男子！彼釋迦牟尼佛於五濁世出現之時，人壽百年，稟性下劣，善根微薄，復無信解。 ^1-349

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-350]]

此諸眾生多有我見、人見、眾生、壽者、養育邪見、我我所見、斷常見等，為欲利益此諸異生及眾外道如是等類，令生正解，速得成就無上菩提，是故釋迦牟尼如來示現如是短促壽命。 ^1-350

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-351]]

善男子！然彼如來欲令眾生見涅槃已，生難遭想、憂苦等想，於佛世尊所說經教，速當受持，讀誦通利，為人解說，不生謗毀，是故如來現斯短壽。 ^1-351

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-352]]

何以故？ ^1-352

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-353]]

彼諸眾生若見如來不般涅槃，不生恭敬難遭之想，如來所說甚深經典，亦不受持讀誦通利、為人宣說。 ^1-353

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-354]]

所以者何？ ^1-354

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-355]]

以常見佛，不尊重故。 ^1-355

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-356]]

善男子！譬如有人見其父母多有財產，珍寶豐盈，便於財物不生希有難遭之想。 ^1-356

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-357]]

所以者何？ ^1-357

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-358]]

於父財物生常想故。 ^1-358

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-359]]

善男子！彼諸眾生亦復如是，若見如來不入涅槃，不生希有難遭之想。 ^1-359

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-360]]

所以者何？ ^1-360

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-361]]

由常見故。 ^1-361

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-362]]

善男子！譬如有人父母貧窮，資財乏少，然彼貧人或詣王家、或大臣舍，見其倉庫種種珍寶悉皆盈滿，生希有心，難遭之想。 ^1-362

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-363]]

時彼貧人為欲求財，廣設方便，策勤無怠。 ^1-363

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-364]]

所以者何？ ^1-364

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-365]]

為捨貧窮，受安樂故。 ^1-365

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-366]]

善男子！彼諸眾生亦復如是，若見如來入於涅槃，生難遭想乃至憂苦等想，復作是念：『於無量劫諸佛如來出現於世，如烏曇跋花，時乃一現。 ^1-366

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-367]]

』彼諸眾生發希有心，起難遭想。 ^1-367

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-368]]

若遇如來，心生敬信，聞說正法，生實語想，所有經典悉皆受持，不生毀謗。 ^1-368

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-369]]

善男子！以是因緣，彼佛世尊，不久住世，速入涅槃。 ^1-369

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-370]]

善男子！是諸如來，以如是等善巧方便成就眾生。 ^1-370

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-371]]

」爾時，四佛說是語已，忽然不現。 ^1-371

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-372]]

_爾時，妙幢菩薩摩訶薩與無量百千菩薩，及無量億那庾多百千眾生，俱共往詣鷲峯山中釋迦牟尼如來正遍知所，頂禮佛足，在一面立。 ^1-372

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-373]]

時妙幢菩薩以如上事具白世尊。 ^1-373

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-374]]

_時四如來亦詣鷲峯，至釋迦牟尼佛所，各隨本方就座而坐，告侍者菩薩言：「善男子！汝今可詣釋迦牟尼佛所，為我致問：『少病少惱，起居輕利，安樂行不？ ^1-374

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-375]]

』復作是言：『善哉！善哉！釋迦牟尼如來！今可演說《金光明經》甚深法要，為欲饒益一切眾生，除去飢饉，令得安樂，我當隨喜。 ^1-375

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-376]]

』」_時彼侍者各詣釋迦牟尼佛所，頂禮雙足，却住一面，俱白佛言：「彼天人師致問無量：『少病少惱，起居輕利，安樂行不？ ^1-376

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-377]]

』復作是言：『善哉！善哉！釋迦牟尼如來！今可演說《金光明經》甚深法要，為欲利益一切眾生，除去飢饉，令得安樂。 ^1-377

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-378]]

』」_爾時，釋迦牟尼如來、應、正等覺告彼侍者諸菩薩言：「善哉！善哉！彼四如來乃能為諸眾生饒益安樂，勸請於我宣揚正法。 ^1-378

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-379]]

」_爾時世尊，而說頌曰：_「我常在鷲山，宣說此經寶；_成就眾生故，示現般涅槃。 ^1-379

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-380]]

_凡夫起邪見，不信我所說；_為成就彼故，示現般涅槃。 ^1-380

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-381]]

」_時大會中有婆羅門，姓憍陳如，名曰法師授記，與無量百千婆羅門眾，供養佛已，聞世尊說入般涅槃，涕淚交流，前禮佛足，白言：「世尊！若實如來於諸眾生有大慈悲，憐愍利益，令得安樂，猶如父母，餘無等者，能與世間作歸依處，如淨滿月，以大智慧能為照明，如日初出，普觀眾生，愛無偏黨，如羅怙羅，惟願世尊施我一願！」_爾時世尊默然而止。 ^1-381

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-382]]

佛威力故，於此眾中，有梨車毘童子，名一切眾生憙見，語婆羅門憍陳如言：「大婆羅門！汝今從佛欲乞何願？ ^1-382

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-383]]

我能與汝。 ^1-383

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-384]]

」_婆羅門言：「童子！我欲供養無上世尊，今從如來求請舍利如芥子許。 ^1-384

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-385]]

何以故？ ^1-385

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-386]]

我曾聞說：『若善男子、善女人得佛舍利如芥子許，恭敬供養，是人當生三十三天而為帝釋。 ^1-386

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-387]]

』」_是時童子語婆羅門曰：「若欲願生三十三天受勝報者，應當至心聽是《金光明最勝王經》。 ^1-387

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-388]]

於諸經中最為殊勝，難解難入，聲聞、獨覺所不能知；此經能生無量無邊福德果報，乃至成辦無上菩提。 ^1-388

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-389]]

我今為汝略說其事。 ^1-389

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-390]]

」_婆羅門言：「善哉！童子！此《金光明》甚深最上，難解難入，聲聞、獨覺尚不能知，何況我等邊鄙之人，智慧微淺而能解了。 ^1-390

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-391]]

是故我今求佛舍利如芥子許，持還本處，置寶函中，恭敬供養，命終之後得為帝釋，常受安樂。 ^1-391

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-392]]

云何汝今不能為我從明行足求斯一願？ ^1-392

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-393]]

」作是語已。 ^1-393

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-394]]

_爾時童子即為婆羅門，而說頌曰：_「恒河駛流水，可生白蓮花；_黃鳥作白形，黑鳥變為赤。 ^1-394

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-395]]

_假使贍部樹，可生多羅果；_朅樹羅枝中，能出菴羅葉。 ^1-395

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-396]]

_斯等希有物，或容可轉變；_世尊之舍利，畢竟不可得。 ^1-396

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-397]]

_假使用龜毛，織成上妙服，_寒時可被著，方求佛舍利。 ^1-397

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-398]]

_假使蚊蚋足，可使成樓觀，_堅固不搖動，方求佛舍利。 ^1-398

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-399]]

_假使水蛭蟲，口中生白齒，_長大利如鋒，方求佛舍利。 ^1-399

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-400]]

_假使持兔角，用成於梯蹬，_可昇上天宮，方求佛舍利。 ^1-400

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-401]]

_鼠緣此梯上，除去阿蘇羅，_能障空中月，方求佛舍利。 ^1-401

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-402]]

_若蠅飲酒醉，周行村邑中，_廣造於舍宅，方求佛舍利。 ^1-402

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-403]]

_若使驢脣色，赤如頻婆果，_善作於歌舞，方求佛舍利。 ^1-403

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-404]]

_烏與鵂鶹鳥，同共一處遊，_彼此相順從，方求佛舍利。 ^1-404

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-405]]

_假使波羅葉，可成於傘蓋，_能遮於大雨，方求佛舍利。 ^1-405

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-406]]

_假令大船舶，盛滿諸財寶，_能令陸地行，方求佛舍利。 ^1-406

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-407]]

_假使鷦鷯鳥，以嘴銜香山，_隨處任遊行，方求佛舍利。 ^1-407

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-408]]

」_爾時，法師授記婆羅門聞此頌已，亦以伽他答一切眾生憙見童子曰：_「善哉大童子，此眾中吉祥；_善巧方便心，得佛無上記。 ^1-408

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-409]]

_如來大威德，能救護世間；_仁可至心聽，我今次第說。 ^1-409

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-410]]

_諸佛境難思，世間無與等；_法身性常住，修行無差別。 ^1-410

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-411]]

_諸佛體皆同，所說法亦爾；_諸佛無作者，亦復本無生。 ^1-411

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-412]]

_世尊金剛體，權現於化身；_是故佛舍利，無如芥子許。 ^1-412

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-413]]

_佛非血肉身，云何有舍利？ ^1-413

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-414]]

_方便留身骨，為益諸眾生。 ^1-414

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-415]]

_法身是正覺，法界即如來；_此是佛真身，亦說如是法。 ^1-415

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-416]]

」_爾時，會中三萬二千天子，聞說如來壽命長遠，皆發阿耨多羅三藐三菩提心，歡喜踊躍，得未曾有。 ^1-416

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-417]]

異口同音，而說頌曰：_「佛不般涅槃，正法亦不滅；_為利眾生故，示現有滅盡。 ^1-417

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-418]]

_世尊不思議，妙體無異相；_為利眾生故，現種種莊嚴。 ^1-418

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-419]]

」_爾時，妙幢菩薩親於佛前，及四如來并二大士諸天子所，聞說釋迦牟尼如來壽量事已，復從座起，合掌恭敬白佛言：「世尊！若實如是，諸佛如來不般涅槃，無舍利者，云何經中說有涅槃及佛舍利，令諸人天恭敬供養？ ^1-419

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-420]]

過去諸佛現有身骨流布於世，人天供養得福無邊？ ^1-420

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-421]]

今復言無，致生疑惑。 ^1-421

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-422]]

惟願世尊哀愍我等，廣為分別！」_爾時，佛告妙幢菩薩及諸大眾：「汝等當知！云般涅槃有舍利者，是密意說，如是之義，當一心聽。 ^1-422

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-423]]

善男子！菩薩摩訶薩如是應知，有其十法能解如來、應、正等覺真實理趣，說有究竟大般涅槃。 ^1-423

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-424]]

云何為十？ ^1-424

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-425]]

一者、諸佛如來究竟斷盡諸煩惱障、所知障故，名為涅槃。 ^1-425

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-426]]

_二者、諸佛如來善能解了有情無性及法無性故，名為涅槃。 ^1-426

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-427]]

_三者、能轉身依及法依故，名為涅槃。 ^1-427

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-428]]

_四者、於諸有情任運休息化因緣故，名為涅槃。 ^1-428

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-429]]

五者、證得真實無差別相平等法身故，名為涅槃。 ^1-429

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-430]]

_六者、了知生死及以涅槃無二性故，名為涅槃。 ^1-430

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-431]]

_七者、於一切法了其根本，證清淨故，名為涅槃。 ^1-431

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-432]]

_八者、於一切法無生無滅善修行故，名為涅槃。 ^1-432

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-433]]

_九者、真如法界實際平等，得正智故，名為涅槃。 ^1-433

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-434]]

_十者、於諸法性及涅槃性，得無差別故，名為涅槃。 ^1-434

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-435]]

是謂十法說有涅槃。 ^1-435

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-436]]

_「復次，善男子！菩薩摩訶薩如是應知，復有十法，能解如來、應、正等覺真實理趣，說有究竟大般涅槃。 ^1-436

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-437]]

_云何為十？ ^1-437

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-438]]

一者、一切煩惱以樂欲為本，從樂欲生；諸佛世尊斷樂欲故，名為涅槃。 ^1-438

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-439]]

_二者、以諸如來斷諸樂欲，不取一法，以不取故，無去無來，無所取故，名為涅槃。 ^1-439

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-440]]

_三者、以無去來及無所取，是則法身不生不滅，無生滅故，名為涅槃。 ^1-440

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-441]]

四者、此無生滅非言所宣，言語斷故，名為涅槃。 ^1-441

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-442]]

_五者、無有我人，惟法生滅，得轉依故，名為涅槃。 ^1-442

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-443]]

_六者、煩惱隨惑，皆是客塵，法性是主，無來無去，佛了知故，名為涅槃。 ^1-443

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-444]]

_七者、真如是實，餘皆虛妄，實性體者即是真如，真如性者即是如來，名為涅槃。 ^1-444

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-445]]

_八者、實際之性無有戲論，惟獨如來證實際法，戲論永斷，名為涅槃。 ^1-445

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-446]]

九者、無生是實，生是虛妄，愚癡之人漂溺生死，如來體實，無有虛妄，名為涅槃。 ^1-446

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-447]]

_十者、不實之法是從緣生，真實之法不從緣起，如來法身，體是真實，名為涅槃。 ^1-447

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-448]]

善男子！是謂十法說有涅槃。 ^1-448

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-449]]

_「復次，善男子！菩薩摩訶薩如是應知，復有十法，能解如來、應、正等覺真實理趣，說有究竟大般涅槃。 ^1-449

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-450]]

云何為十？ ^1-450

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-451]]

_一者、如來善知施及施果，無我我所，此施及果不正分別，永除滅故，名為涅槃。 ^1-451

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-452]]

_二者、如來善知戒及戒果，無我我所，此戒及果不正分別，永除滅故，名為涅槃。 ^1-452

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-453]]

_三者、如來善知忍及忍果，無我我所，此忍及果不正分別，永除滅故，名為涅槃。 ^1-453

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-454]]

_四者、如來善知勤及勤果，無我我所，此勤及果不正分別，永除滅故，名為涅槃。 ^1-454

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-455]]

_五者、如來善知定及定果，無我我所，此定及果不正分別，永除滅故，名為涅槃。 ^1-455

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-456]]

_六者、如來善知慧及慧果，無我我所，此慧及果不正分別，永除滅故，名為涅槃。 ^1-456

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-457]]

_七者、諸佛如來善能了知一切有情非有情，一切諸法皆無性，不正分別，永除滅故，名為涅槃。 ^1-457

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-458]]

_八者、若自愛者便起追求，由追求故，受眾苦惱；諸佛如來除自愛故，永絕追求，無追求故，名為涅槃。 ^1-458

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-459]]

_九者、有為之法皆有數量，無為法者數量皆除，佛離有為，證無為法，無數量故，名為涅槃。 ^1-459

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-460]]

_十者、如來了知有情及法，體性皆空，離空非有，空性即是真法身故，名為涅槃。 ^1-460

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-461]]

善男子！是謂十法說有涅槃。 ^1-461

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-462]]

_「復次，善男子！豈惟如來不般涅槃是為希有？ ^1-462

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-463]]

_復有十種希有之法，是如來行。 ^1-463

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-464]]

云何為十？ ^1-464

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-465]]

_一者、生死過失，涅槃寂靜，由於生死及以涅槃證平等故，不處流轉，不住涅槃，於諸有情不生厭背，是如來行。 ^1-465

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-466]]

_二者、佛於眾生不作是念：『此諸愚夫行顛倒見，為諸煩惱之所纏迫；我今開悟，令其解脫。 ^1-466

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-467]]

』然由往昔慈善根力，於彼有情隨其根性意樂勝解，不起分別，任運濟度，示教利喜，盡未來際無有窮盡，是如來行。 ^1-467

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-468]]

_三者、佛無是念：『我今演說十二分教，利益有情。 ^1-468

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-469]]

』然由往昔慈善根力，為彼有情廣說，乃至盡未來際無有窮盡，是如來行。 ^1-469

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-470]]

_四者、佛無是念：『我今往彼城邑聚落，王及大臣、婆羅門、剎帝利、薜舍、戍達羅等舍，從其乞食。 ^1-470

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-471]]

』然由往昔身語意行慣習力故，任運詣彼，為利益事而行乞食，是如來行。 ^1-471

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-472]]

_五者、如來之身無有饑渴，亦無便利羸憊之相，雖行乞取而無所食，亦無分別，然為任運利益有情，示有食相，是如來行。 ^1-472

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-473]]

_六者、佛無是念：『此諸眾生，有上、中、下，隨彼機性而為說法。 ^1-473

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-474]]

』然佛世尊無有分別，隨其器量善應機緣，為彼說法，是如來行。 ^1-474

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-475]]

_七者、佛無是念：『此類有情不恭敬我，常於我所出呵罵言，不能與彼共為言論；彼類有情恭敬於我，常於我所共相讚歎，我當與彼共為言說。 ^1-475

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-476]]

』然而如來起慈悲心，平等無二，是如來行。 ^1-476

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-477]]

_八者、諸佛如來無有愛憎、憍慢、貪惜及諸煩惱，然而如來常樂寂靜，讚歎少欲，離諸諠閙，是如來行。 ^1-477

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-478]]

_九者、如來無有一法不知、不善通達，於一切處鏡智現前無有分別。 ^1-478

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-479]]

然而如來，見彼有情所作事業，隨彼意轉，方便誘引，令得出離，是如來行。 ^1-479

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-480]]

_十者、如來若見一分有情得富盛時，不生歡喜；見其衰損，不起憂慼。 ^1-480

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-481]]

然而如來見彼有情修習正行，無礙大慈自然救攝；若見有情修習邪行，無礙大悲自然救攝。 ^1-481

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-482]]

是如來行。 ^1-482

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-483]]

_善男子！如是當知如來、應、正等覺，說有如是無邊正行。 ^1-483

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-484]]

汝等當知，是謂涅槃真實之相。 ^1-484

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-485]]

或時見有般涅槃者，是權方便，及留舍利令諸有情恭敬供養，皆是如來慈善根力。 ^1-485

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-486]]

若供養者，於未來世，遠離八難，逢值諸佛，遇善知識，不失善心，福報無邊，速當出離，不為生死之所纏縛，如是妙行，汝等勤修，勿為放逸。 ^1-486

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-487]]

」_爾時，妙幢菩薩聞佛親說不般涅槃及甚深行，合掌恭敬白言：「我今始知如來大師不般涅槃，及留舍利普益眾生，身心踊悅，歎未曾有。 ^1-487

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-488]]

」說是如來壽量品時，無量無數無邊眾生，皆發無等等阿耨多羅三藐三菩提心。 ^1-488

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-489]]

時四如來忽然不現，妙幢菩薩，禮佛足已，從座而起還其本處。 ^1-489

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-490]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-1521]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-1525]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-1914]]

悉皆迴向阿耨多羅三藐三菩提。 」 ^1-1916

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-1918]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-1921]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-1928]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-1934]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-1935]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-1937]]

如是過去、未來一切眾生， ^1-1937

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-1938]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-1939]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-1940]]

過百大劫行菩薩行， ^1-1940

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-1941]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-1942]]

 ^1-3384

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3442]]

未生諸法即是非有， ^1-3442

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3443]]

無名無相， ^1-3443

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3444]]

非校量譬喻之所能及， ^1-3444

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3445]]

非是因緣之所生故。 ^1-3445

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3446]]

善女天！ ^1-3446

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3447]]

譬如鼓聲，依木、 ^1-3447

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3448]]

依皮及桴手等故得出聲， ^1-3448

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3449]]

如是鼓聲過去亦空， ^1-3449

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3450]]

未來亦空， ^1-3450

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3451]]

現在亦空。 ^1-3451

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3452]]

何以故？ ^1-3452

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3453]]

是鼓音聲不從木生， ^1-3453

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3454]]

不從皮生 ^1-3454

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3455]]

及桴手生， ^1-3455

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3456]]

不於三世生， ^1-3456

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3457]]

是則不生。 ^1-3457

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3458]]

若不可生，則不可滅； ^1-3458

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3459]]

若不可滅，無所從來； ^1-3459

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3460]]

若無所從來，亦無所去； ^1-3460

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3461]]

若無所去， ^1-3461

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3462]]

則非常非斷； ^1-3462

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3463]]

若非常非斷， ^1-3463

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3464]]

則不一不異。 ^1-3464

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3465]]

何以故？ ^1-3465

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3466]]

此若是一， ^1-3466

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3467]]

則不異法界。 ^1-3467

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3468]]

若如是者， ^1-3468

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3469]]

凡夫之人應見真諦， ^1-3469

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3470]]

得於無上安樂涅槃； ^1-3470

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3471]]

既不如是，故知不一。 ^1-3471

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3472]]

若言異者， ^1-3472

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3473]]

一切諸佛菩薩行相即是執著， ^1-3473

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3474]]

未得解脫煩惱繫縛， ^1-3474

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3475]]

即不證於阿耨多羅三藐三菩提。 ^1-3475

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3476]]

何以故？ ^1-3476

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3477]]

一切聖人於行非行， ^1-3477

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3478]]

同真實性， ^1-3478

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3479]]

是故不異。 ^1-3479

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3480]]

故知五蘊非有非無， ^1-3480

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3481]]

不從因緣生， ^1-3481

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3482]]

非無因緣生， ^1-3482

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3483]]

是聖所知， ^1-3483

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3484]]

非餘境故， ^1-3484

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3485]]

亦非言說之所能及， ^1-3485

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3486]]

無名無相， ^1-3486

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3487]]

無因無緣， ^1-3487

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3488]]

亦無譬喻， ^1-3488

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3489]]

始終寂靜， ^1-3489

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3490]]

本來自空。 ^1-3490

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3491]]

是故五蘊能現法界。 ^1-3491

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3492]]

善女天！若善男子、 ^1-3492

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3493]]

善女人欲求阿耨多羅三藐三菩提， ^1-3493

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3494]]

異真異俗， ^1-3494

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3495]]

難可思量， ^1-3495

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3496]]

於凡聖境， ^1-3496

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3497]]

體非一異， ^1-3497

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3498]]

不捨於俗， ^1-3498

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3499]]

不離於真， ^1-3499

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3500]]

依於法界， ^1-3500

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3501]]

行菩提行。 」 ^1-3501

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3502]]

爾時，世尊作是語已， ^1-3502

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3503]]

時善女天踊躍歡喜， ^1-3503

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3504]]

即從座起， ^1-3504

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3505]]

偏袒右肩， ^1-3505

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3506]]

右膝著地， ^1-3506

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3507]]

合掌恭敬， ^1-3507

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3508]]

一心頂禮， ^1-3508

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3509]]

而白佛言： ^1-3509

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3510]]

「世尊！如上所說菩提正行， ^1-3510

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3511]]

我今當學。」 ^1-3511

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3512]]

是時索訶世界主大梵天王於大眾中， ^1-3512

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3513]]

問如意寶光耀善女天曰： ^1-3513

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3514]]

「此菩提行難可修行， ^1-3514

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3515]]

汝今云何於菩提行而得自在？」 ^1-3515

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3516]]

爾時，善女天答梵王曰： ^1-3516

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3517]]

「大梵王！如佛所說， ^1-3517

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3518]]

實是甚深。 ^1-3518

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3519]]

一切異生不解其義， ^1-3519

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3520]]

是聖境界微妙難知。 ^1-3520

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3521]]

若使我今依於此法 ^1-3521

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3522]]

得安樂住， ^1-3522

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3523]]

是實語者， ^1-3523

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3524]]

願令一切五濁惡世無量無數無邊眾生， ^1-3524

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3525]]

皆得金色三十二相， ^1-3525

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3526]]

非男非女， ^1-3526

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3527]]

坐寶蓮花， ^1-3527

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3528]]

受無量樂， ^1-3528

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3529]]

雨天妙花， ^1-3529

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3530]]

諸天音樂不鼓自鳴， ^1-3530

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3531]]

一切供養皆悉具足。 」 ^1-3531

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3532]]

時善女天說是語已， ^1-3532

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3533]]

一切五濁惡世所有眾生皆悉金色， ^1-3533

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3534]]

具大人相， ^1-3534

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3535]]

非男非女， ^1-3535

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3536]]

坐寶蓮花， ^1-3536

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3537]]

受無量樂， ^1-3537

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3538]]

猶如他化自在天宮， ^1-3538

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3539]]

無諸惡道， ^1-3539

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3540]]

寶樹行列， ^1-3540

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3541]]

七寶蓮花遍滿世界， ^1-3541

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3542]]

又雨七寶上妙天花， ^1-3542

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3543]]

作天伎樂。 ^1-3543

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3544]]

如意寶光耀善女天即轉女身， ^1-3544

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3545]]

作梵天身。 ^1-3545

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3546]]

時大梵王問如意寶光耀菩薩言： ^1-3546

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3547]]

「仁者！如何行菩提行？ 」 ^1-3547

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3548]]

答言：「梵王！若水中月行菩提行， ^1-3548

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3549]]

我亦行菩提行； ^1-3549

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3550]]

若夢中行菩提行， ^1-3550

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3551]]

我亦行菩提行； ^1-3551

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3552]]

若陽焰行菩提行， ^1-3552

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3553]]

我亦行菩提行； ^1-3553

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3554]]

若谷響行菩提行， ^1-3554

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3555]]

我亦行菩提行。 」 ^1-3555

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3556]]

時大梵王聞此說已， ^1-3556

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3557]]

白菩薩言： ^1-3557

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3558]]

「仁依何義而說此語？ 」 ^1-3558

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3559]]

答言：「梵王！無有一法是實相者， ^1-3559

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3560]]

但由因緣而得成故。 」 ^1-3560

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3561]]

梵王言：「若如是者， ^1-3561

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3562]]

諸凡夫人皆悉應得阿耨多羅三藐三菩提。 」 ^1-3562

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3563]]

答言：「仁以何意而作是說？ ^1-3563

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3564]]

愚癡人異， ^1-3564

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3565]]

智慧人異？ ^1-3565

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3566]]

菩提異， ^1-3566

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3567]]

非菩提異？ ^1-3567

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3568]]

解脫異， ^1-3568

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3569]]

非解脫異？ ^1-3569

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3570]]

梵王！如是諸法， ^1-3570

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3571]]

平等無異。 ^1-3571

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3572]]

於此法界真如， ^1-3572

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3573]]

不一不異， ^1-3573

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3574]]

無有中間而可執著， ^1-3574

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3575]]

無增無減。 ^1-3575

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3576]]

梵王！譬如幻師 ^1-3576

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3577]]

及幻弟子善解幻術， ^1-3577

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3578]]

於四衢道， ^1-3578

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3579]]

取諸沙土草木葉等， ^1-3579

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3580]]

聚在一處 ^1-3580

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3581]]

作諸幻術， 使人覩見象眾、馬眾、車兵等眾， ^1-3581

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3582]]

七寶之聚種種倉庫。 ^1-3582

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3583]]

 ^1-3583

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3584]]

若有眾生愚癡無智， ^1-3584

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3585]]

不能思惟， ^1-3585

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3586]]

不知幻本， ^1-3586

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3587]]

若見若聞， ^1-3587

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3588]]

作是思惟： 『我所見聞象、馬等眾， ^1-3588

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3589]]

此是實有， ^1-3589

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3590]]

餘皆虛妄。 』 ^1-3590

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3591]]

於後更不審察思惟。 ^1-3591

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3592]]

有智之人則不如是， ^1-3592

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3593]]

了於幻本， ^1-3593

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3594]]

若見若聞， ^1-3594

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3595]]

作如是念： ^1-3595

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3596]]

『如我所見象、馬等眾， ^1-3596

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3597]]

非是真實，唯有幻事， ^1-3597

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3598]]

惑人眼目， ^1-3598

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3599]]

妄謂象等及諸倉庫， ^1-3599

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3600]]

有名無實， ^1-3600

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3601]]

如我見聞， ^1-3601

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3602]]

不執為實。 』 ^1-3602

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3603]]

後時思惟， ^1-3603

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3604]]

知其虛妄。 ^1-3604

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3605]]

是故智者了一切法皆無實體， ^1-3605

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3606]]

但隨世俗，如見如聞， ^1-3606

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3607]]

表宣其事， ^1-3607

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3608]]

思惟諦理， ^1-3608

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3609]]

則不如是， ^1-3609

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3610]]

復由假說， ^1-3610

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3611]]

顯實義故。 ^1-3611

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3612]]

「梵王！愚癡異生， ^1-3612

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3613]]

未得出世聖慧之眼， ^1-3613

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3614]]

未知一切諸法真如不可說故。 ^1-3614

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3615]]

是諸凡愚， ^1-3615

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3616]]

若見若聞行非行法， ^1-3616

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3617]]

如是思惟， ^1-3617

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3618]]

便生執著， ^1-3618

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3619]]

謂以為實，於第一義不能了知諸法真如是不可說。 ^1-3619

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3620]]

是諸聖人， ^1-3620

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3621]]

若見若聞行非行法， ^1-3621

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3622]]

隨其力能不生執著以為實有， ^1-3622

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3623]]

了知一切無實行法、無實非行法， ^1-3623

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3624]]

但妄思量行非行相， ^1-3624

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3625]]

唯有名字， ^1-3625

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3626]]

無有實體。 ^1-3626

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3627]]

是諸聖人， ^1-3627

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3628]]

隨世俗說， ^1-3628

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3629]]

為欲令他知真實義。 ^1-3629

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3630]]

如是梵王！是諸聖人， ^1-3630

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3631]]

以聖智見， ^1-3631

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3632]]

了法真如不可說故， ^1-3632

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3633]]

行非行法亦復如是， ^1-3633

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3634]]

令他證知，故說種種世俗名言。 」 ^1-3634

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3635]]

時大梵王問如意寶光耀菩薩言： ^1-3635

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3636]]

「有幾眾生能解如是甚深正法？ 」 ^1-3636

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3637]]

答言：「梵王！ ^1-3637

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3638]]

有眾幻人心心數法， ^1-3638

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3639]]

能解如是甚深正法。 」 ^1-3639

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3640]]

梵王曰：「此幻化人， ^1-3640

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3641]]

體是非有， ^1-3641

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3642]]

此之心數從何而生？ 」 ^1-3642

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3643]]

答曰：「若知法界不有不無， ^1-3643

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3644]]

如是眾生能解深義。 」 ^1-3644

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3645]]

爾時，梵王白佛言： ^1-3645

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3646]]

「世尊！是如意寶光耀菩薩不可思議， ^1-3646

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3647]]

通達如是甚深之義。 」 ^1-3647

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3648]]

佛言：「如是！如是！ ^1-3648

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3649]]

梵王！如汝所言， ^1-3649

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3650]]

此如意寶光耀， ^1-3650

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3651]]

已教汝等發心修學無生忍法。 」 ^1-3651

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3652]]

是時大梵天王與諸梵眾， ^1-3652

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3653]]

從座而起， ^1-3653

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3654]]

偏袒右肩， ^1-3654

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3655]]

合掌恭敬， ^1-3655

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3656]]

頂禮如意寶光耀菩薩足， ^1-3656

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3657]]

作如是言：「希有！希有！ ^1-3657

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3658]]

我等今日幸遇大士， 得聞正法。 」 ^1-3658

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3659]]

爾時，世尊告梵王言： ^1-3659

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3660]]

「是如意寶光耀， ^1-3660

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3661]]

於未來世當得作佛，號寶焰吉祥藏如來、應、正遍知、明行圓滿、善逝、世間解、無上士、調御丈夫、天人師、佛、世尊。 」 ^1-3661

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3662]]

說是品時， ^1-3662

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3663]]

有三千億菩薩於阿耨多羅三藐三菩提得不退轉， ^1-3663

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3664]]

八千億天子、無量無數國王臣民遠塵離垢， ^1-3664

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3665]]

得法眼淨。 ^1-3665

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3666]]

爾時，會中有五十億苾芻， ^1-3666

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3667]]

行菩薩行， ^1-3667

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3668]]

欲退菩提心， ^1-3668

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3669]]

聞如意寶光耀菩薩說是法時， ^1-3669

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3670]]

皆得堅固不可思議滿足上願， ^1-3670

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3671]]

更復發起菩提之心， ^1-3671

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3672]]

各自脫衣供養菩薩， ^1-3672

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3673]]

重發無上勝進之心， ^1-3673

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3674]]

作如是願： ^1-3674

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3675]]

「願令我等功德善根悉皆不退，迴向阿耨多羅三藐三菩提。 」 ^1-3675

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3676]]

「梵王！是諸苾芻， ^1-3676

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3677]]

依此功德如說修行， ^1-3677

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3678]]

過九十大劫， ^1-3678

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3679]]

當得解悟， ^1-3679

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3680]]

出離生死。 」 ^1-3680

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3681]]

爾時，世尊即為授記： ^1-3681

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3682]]

「汝諸苾芻過三十阿僧祇劫， ^1-3682

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3683]]

當得作佛， ^1-3683

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3684]]

劫名難勝光王， ^1-3684

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3685]]

國名無垢光， ^1-3685

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3686]]

同時皆得阿耨多羅三藐三菩提， ^1-3686

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3687]]

皆同一號，名願莊嚴間飾王， ^1-3687

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3688]]

十號具足。 ^1-3688

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3689]]

「梵王！是《金光明》微妙經典， ^1-3689

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3690]]

若正聞持， ^1-3690

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3691]]

有大威力。 ^1-3691

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3692]]

假使有人於百千大劫， ^1-3692

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3693]]

行六波羅蜜無有方便； ^1-3693

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3694]]

若有善男子、善女人書寫如是《金光明經》， ^1-3694

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3695]]

半月半月專心讀誦， ^1-3695

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3696]]

是功德聚於前功德百分不及一， ^1-3696

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3697]]

乃至算數譬喻所不能及。 ^1-3697

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3698]]

梵王！是故我今令汝修學， ^1-3698

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3699]]

憶念受持， ^1-3699

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3700]]

為他廣說。 ^1-3700

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3701]]

何以故？ ^1-3701

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3702]]

我於往昔行菩薩道時， ^1-3702

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3703]]

猶如勇士入於戰陣， ^1-3703

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3704]]

不惜身命， ^1-3704

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3705]]

流通如是微妙經王， ^1-3705

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-3706]]

受持讀誦， ^1-3706

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4614]]

勃陀(引)也_ ^1-4614

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4615]]

南謨薜室囉末拏也_ ^1-4615

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4616]]

藥叉囉闍(引)也_ ^1-4616

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4617]]

莫訶囉闍 ^1-4617

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4618]]

阿地囉闍也_ ^1-4618

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4619]]

南麼室唎耶裔_ 莫訶提弊(引)裔_ ^1-4619

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4620]]

怛姪他_ ^1-4620

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4621]]

怛囉怛囉_ ^1-4621

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4622]]

咄嚕咄嚕_ ^1-4622

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4623]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4624]]

𡨧卛吐𡨧卛吐_ ^1-4624

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4625]]

漢娜漢娜_ ^1-4625

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4626]]

末尼羯諾迦_ ^1-4626

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4627]]

跋折囉薜琉璃也_ ^1-4627

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4628]]

目底迦楞訖㗚哆_ ^1-4628

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4629]]

設唎囉裔_ ^1-4629

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4630]]

蒲(引)薩婆薩埵呬哆迦摩 ^1-4630

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4631]]

薜室囉末拏_ ^1-4631

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4632]]

室唎夜提鼻_ 跋𡅘婆也_ ^1-4632

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4633]]

醫呬醫呬 ^1-4633

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4634]]

磨毘藍婆_ ^1-4634

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4635]]

瞿㗚拏瞿㗚拏 ^1-4635

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4636]]

𧙕(麻八反) 喇娑 ^1-4636

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4637]]

𧙕喇娑_ ^1-4637

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4638]]

達䭾呬麼麼_阿目迦那末寫 ^1-4638

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4639]]

(自稱己名) ^1-4639

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4640]]

達哩設那迦末寫達哩設南_ ^1-4640

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4641]]

麼麼末那_ ^1-4641

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4642]]

鉢喇曷囉大也_莎訶_ ^1-4642

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4643]]

「世尊！ ^1-4643

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4644]]

我若見此誦呪之人，復見如是盛興供養， ^1-4644

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4645]]

即生慈愛歡喜之心。 ^1-4645

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4646]]

我即變身作小兒形，或作老人、苾芻之像， ^1-4646

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4647]]

手持如意末尼寶珠， 并持金囊 ^1-4647

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4648]]

入道場內， ^1-4648

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4649]]

身現恭敬， ^1-4649

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4650]]

口稱佛名， ^1-4650

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4651]]

語持呪者曰：『隨汝所求，皆令如願。 』 ^1-4651

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4652]]

或隱林藪， ^1-4652

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4653]]

或造寶珠， ^1-4653

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4654]]

或欲眾人愛寵， ^1-4654

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4655]]

或求金銀等物， ^1-4655

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4656]]

欲持諸呪，皆令有驗。 ^1-4656

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4657]]

或欲神通壽命長遠， ^1-4657

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4658]]

及勝妙樂， ^1-4658

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4659]]

無不稱心。 ^1-4659

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4660]]

我今且說如是之事， ^1-4660

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4661]]

若更求餘， ^1-4661

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4662]]

皆隨所願， 悉得成就， ^1-4662

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4663]]

寶藏無盡， ^1-4663

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4664]]

功德無窮。 ^1-4664

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4665]]

假使日月墜墮于地， ^1-4665

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4666]]

或可大地有時移轉， ^1-4666

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4667]]

我此實語終不虛然， ^1-4667

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4668]]

常得安隱，隨心快樂。 ^1-4668

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4669]]

世尊！ ^1-4669

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4670]]

若有人能受持讀誦是經王者， ^1-4670

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4671]]

誦此呪時， ^1-4671

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4672]]

不假疲勞，法速成就。 ^1-4672

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4673]]

世尊！ ^1-4673

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4674]]

我今為彼貧窮困厄苦惱眾生， ^1-4674

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4675]]

說此神呪， ^1-4675

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4676]]

令獲大利， ^1-4676

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4677]]

皆得富樂， ^1-4677

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4678]]

自在無患， ^1-4678

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4679]]

乃至盡形， ^1-4679

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4680]]

我當擁護， 隨逐是人， ^1-4680

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4681]]

為除災厄。 ^1-4681

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4682]]

亦復令此持《金光明最勝王經》 ^1-4682

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4683]]

流通之者， ^1-4683

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4684]]

及持呪人， ^1-4684

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4685]]

於百步內光明照燭， ^1-4685

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4686]]

我之所有千藥叉神亦常侍衛， ^1-4686

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4687]]

隨欲驅使， ^1-4687

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4688]]

無不遂心。 ^1-4688

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4689]]

我說實語， 無有虛誑， ^1-4689

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4690]]

惟佛證知。 」_ ^1-4690

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4691]]

時多聞天王說此呪已， ^1-4691

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4692]]

佛言：「 ^1-4692

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4693]]

{} 天王！ ^1-4693

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4694]]

汝能破裂一切眾生貧窮苦網， ^1-4694

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4695]]

令得富樂， ^1-4695

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4696]]

{復令此經廣行於世。} ^1-4696

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4697]]

說是神呪， ^1-4697

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4698]]

{善哉！} {} 」_ ^1-4698

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4699]]

時四天王俱從座起， ^1-4699

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4700]]

偏袒一肩， ^1-4700

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4701]]

頂禮雙足， ^1-4701

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4702]]

右膝著地， ^1-4702

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4703]]

合掌恭敬， ^1-4703

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4704]]

以妙伽他讚佛功德：_「 ^1-4704

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4705]]

佛面猶如淨滿月， ^1-4705

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4706]]

亦如千日放光明；_ ^1-4706

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4707]]

目淨脩廣若青蓮， ^1-4707

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4708]]

齒白齊密猶珂雪。 _ ^1-4708

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4709]]

佛德無邊如大海， ^1-4709

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4710]]

無限妙寶積其中；_ ^1-4710

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4711]]

智慧德水鎮恒盈， ^1-4711

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4712]]

百千勝定咸充滿。 _ ^1-4712

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4713]]

足下輪相皆嚴飾， ^1-4713

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4714]]

轂輞千輻悉齊平；_ ^1-4714

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4715]]

手足鞔網遍莊嚴， ^1-4715

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4716]]

猶如鵝王相具足。 _ ^1-4716

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4717]]

佛身光曜等金山， ^1-4717

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4718]]

清淨殊特無倫匹；_ ^1-4718

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4719]]

亦如妙高功德滿， ^1-4719

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4720]]

故我稽首佛山王。 _ ^1-4720

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4721]]

相好如空不可測， ^1-4721

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4722]]

逾於千月放光明；_ ^1-4722

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4723]]

皆如焰幻不思議， ^1-4723

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4724]]

故我稽首心無著。 」_ ^1-4724

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4725]]

爾時，四天王讚歎佛已， ^1-4725

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4726]]

世尊亦以伽他而答之曰：_「 ^1-4726

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4727]]

此《金光明》最勝經， ^1-4727

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4728]]

無上十力之所說；_ ^1-4728

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4729]]

汝等四王常擁護， ^1-4729

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4730]]

應生勇猛不退心。 _ ^1-4730

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4731]]

此妙經寶極甚深， ^1-4731

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4732]]

能與一切有情樂；_ ^1-4732

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4733]]

由彼有情安樂故， ^1-4733

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4734]]

常得流通贍部洲。 _ ^1-4734

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4735]]

於此大千世界中， ^1-4735

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4736]]

所有一切有情類；_ ^1-4736

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4737]]

餓鬼傍生及地獄， ^1-4737

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4738]]

如是苦趣悉皆除。 _ ^1-4738

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4739]]

住此南洲諸國王， ^1-4739

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4740]]

及餘一切有情類；_ ^1-4740

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4741]]

由經威力常歡喜， ^1-4741

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4742]]

皆蒙擁護得安寧。 _ ^1-4742

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4743]]

亦使此中諸有情， ^1-4743

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4744]]

除眾病苦無賊盜；_ ^1-4744

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4800]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4801]]

 ^1-4823

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4825]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4831]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4836]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4837]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4840]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4841]]

非法、 ^1-4841

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4842]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4843]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4844]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4845]]

非現在， ^1-4845

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4846]]

非事、 ^1-4846

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4847]]

非非事， ^1-4847

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4848]]

非緣、 ^1-4848

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4849]]

非非緣， ^1-4849

非非行， ^1-4851

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4852]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4853]]

亦無法滅。 ^1-4853

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4854]]

然為利益諸菩薩故，作如是說。 ^1-4854

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4855]]

於此陀羅尼功用正道理趣勢力安立， ^1-4855

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4856]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4859]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4860]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4861]]

「世尊！惟願善逝為我說此陀羅尼法。 ^1-4864

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4873]]

如汝所說。 ^1-4873

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4874]]

若有菩薩得此陀羅尼者， ^1-4874

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-4875]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5003]]

哀愍世間，擁護一切， ^1-5004

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5005]]

有大威力， ^1-5005

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5006]]

所求如願。 」 ^1-5006

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5007]]

即說呪曰： ^1-5007

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5008]]

「怛姪他_醯哩_弭哩_地哩_莎訶_跋囉甜魔布囇_跋囉甜麼末泥_跋囉甜麼揭鞞_補澁跛僧悉怛囇_莎訶_ ^1-5008

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5009]]

「世尊！我此神呪名曰梵治， ^1-5009

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5012]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5013]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5014]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5015]]

白佛言： ^1-5017

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5018]]

「世尊！我亦有陀羅尼， ^1-5018

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5019]]

名跋折羅扇儞， 是大明呪， ^1-5019

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5024]]

利益人天。 」 ^1-5024

即說呪曰： ^1-5025

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5026]]

「怛姪他_毘儞_婆喇儞_畔陀麼彈滯磨膩儞㨖_爾瞿哩_健陀哩_旃茶哩_摩登耆(上)卜羯死_薩囉跋𡂖鞞(去)_呬娜末低_答麼_嗢多喇儞_莫呼喇儞_達喇儞_計斫羯囉婆抧_捨伐哩_奢伐哩_莎訶」 ^1-5026

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5027]]

爾時，多聞天王、 ^1-5027

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5028]]

持國天王、 ^1-5028

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5029]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5030]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5031]]

合掌恭敬， ^1-5031

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5032]]

白佛言： ^1-5032

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5033]]

「世尊！我今亦有神呪， ^1-5033

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5034]]

名施一切眾生無畏， ^1-5034

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5035]]

於諸苦惱常為擁護， ^1-5035

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5036]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5037]]
