![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5038]]

無諸患苦， ^1-5038

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5039]]

乃至抂死， ^1-5039

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5040]]

悉皆遠離。 」 ^1-5040

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5041]]

即說呪曰： ^1-5041

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5042]]

「怛姪他補澁閉_蘇補澁閉_度麼鉢唎呵囇_阿囄耶鉢喇設悉帝_扇帝涅_目帝忙揭例窣覩帝_悉哆鼻帝_莎訶」 ^1-5042

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5043]]

爾時，復有諸大龍王， ^1-5043

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5044]]

所謂末那斯龍王、 ^1-5044

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5045]]

電光龍王、 ^1-5045

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5046]]

無熱池龍王、 ^1-5046

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5047]]

電舌龍王、 ^1-5047

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5048]]

妙光龍王，俱從座起， ^1-5048

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5049]]

合掌恭敬， ^1-5049

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5050]]

白佛言： ^1-5050

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5051]]

「世尊！我亦有如意寶珠陀羅尼， ^1-5051

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5052]]

能遮惡電， ^1-5052

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5053]]

除諸恐怖， ^1-5053

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5054]]

能於人天為大利益， ^1-5054

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5055]]

哀愍世間， ^1-5055

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5056]]

擁護一切， ^1-5056

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5057]]

有大威力， ^1-5057

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5058]]

所求如願， ^1-5058

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5059]]

乃至抂死， ^1-5059

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5060]]

悉皆遠離， ^1-5060

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5061]]

一切毒藥皆令止息， ^1-5061

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5062]]

一切造作蠱道呪術不吉祥事 ^1-5062

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5063]]

悉令除滅。 ^1-5063

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5064]]

我今以此神呪奉獻世尊， ^1-5064

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5065]]

惟願哀愍慈悲納受， ^1-5065

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5066]]

當令我等離此龍趣 ^1-5066

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5067]]

永捨慳貪。 ^1-5067

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5068]]

何以故？ ^1-5068

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5069]]

由此慳貪於生死中受諸苦惱， ^1-5069

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5070]]

我等願斷慳貪種子。 」 ^1-5070

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5071]]

即說呪曰： ^1-5071

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5072]]

「怛姪他_阿折囇_阿末囇_阿蜜㗚諦_惡叉裔_阿弊裔_奔尼鉢唎耶栗諦_薩婆波跛_鉢喇苫摩尼裔_莎訶_阿離裔般豆_蘇波尼裔莎訶_ ^1-5072

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5073]]

「世尊！若有善男子、善女人， ^1-5073

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5074]]

口中說此陀羅尼明呪， ^1-5074

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5075]]

或書經卷， ^1-5075

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5076]]

受持讀誦、 ^1-5076

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5077]]

恭敬供養者， ^1-5077

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5078]]

終無雷電霹靂， ^1-5078

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5079]]

及諸恐怖苦惱憂患， ^1-5079

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5080]]

乃至抂死， ^1-5080

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5081]]

悉皆遠離； ^1-5081

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5082]]

所有毒藥蠱魅厭禱， ^1-5082

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5083]]

害人虎狼師子毒蛇之類， ^1-5083

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5084]]

乃至蚊虻悉不為害。 」 ^1-5084

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5085]]

爾時，世尊普告大眾：「善哉！善哉！ ^1-5085

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5086]]

此等神呪皆有大力， ^1-5086

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5087]]

能隨眾生心所求事，悉令圓滿， ^1-5087

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5088]]

為大利益， ^1-5088

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5089]]

除不至心； ^1-5089

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5090]]

汝等勿疑。 」 ^1-5090

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5091]]

時諸大眾聞佛語已， ^1-5091

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5092]]

 ^1-5092

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5093]]

歡喜信受。 ^1-5093

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5094]]

 ^1-5094

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5095]]

爾時大辯才天女於大眾中，即從座起，頂禮佛足，白佛言： ^1-5095

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5096]]

「世尊！若有法師，說是《金光明最勝王經》者， ^1-5096

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5097]]

我當益其智慧， ^1-5097

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5098]]

具足莊嚴言說之辯； ^1-5098

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5099]]

若彼法師於此經中文字句義所有忘失， ^1-5099

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5100]]

皆令憶持， ^1-5100

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5101]]

能善開悟， ^1-5101

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5102]]

復與陀羅尼總持無礙。 ^1-5102

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5103]]

又此《金光明最勝王經》，為彼有情已於百千佛所種諸善根常受持者， ^1-5103

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5104]]

於贍部洲廣行流布， ^1-5104

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5105]]

不速隱沒。 ^1-5105

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5106]]

復令無量有情聞是經典，皆得不可思議捷利辯才無盡大慧， ^1-5106

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5107]]

善解眾論及諸伎術， ^1-5107

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5108]]

能出生死， ^1-5108

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5109]]

速趣無上正等菩提， ^1-5109

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5110]]

於現世中增益壽命，資身之具悉令圓滿。 ^1-5110

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5111]]

世尊！我當為彼持經法師及餘有情，於此經典樂聽聞者， ^1-5111

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5112]]

說其呪藥洗浴之法。 ^1-5112

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5113]]

彼人所有惡星災變與初生時星屬相違， ^1-5113

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5114]]

疫病之苦、 ^1-5114

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5115]]

鬪諍戰陣、 ^1-5115

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5116]]

惡夢鬼神、蠱毒厭魅、呪術起屍， ^1-5116

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5117]]

如是諸惡為障難者，悉令除滅。 ^1-5117

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5118]]

諸有智者應作如是洗浴之法， ^1-5118

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5119]]

當取香藥三十二味， ^1-5119

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5120]]

所謂：「菖蒲(跋者)、牛黃(瞿盧折娜)、苜蓿香(塞畢力迦)、麝香(莫訶婆伽)、雄黃(末㮈眵羅)、合昬樹(尸利灑)、白及(因達囉喝悉哆)、芎藭(闍莫迦)、㺃杞根(苫弭)、松脂(室利薜瑟得迦)、桂皮(咄者)、香附子(目窣哆)、沈香(惡揭嚕)、栴檀(栴檀娜)、零凌香(多揭羅)、丁子(索瞿者)、欝金(茶矩麼)、婆律膏(揭羅娑)、葦香(捺剌柁)、竹黃(𩾲路戰娜)、細豆蔻(蘇泣迷羅)、甘松(苦弭哆)、藿香(鉢怛羅)、茅根香(嗢尸羅)、叱脂(薩洛計)、艾納(世黎也)、安息香(窶具攞)、芥子(薩利殺跛)、馬芹(葉婆儞)、龍花鬚(那伽雞薩羅)、白膠(薩折羅婆)、青木(矩瑟侘)。 ^1-5120

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5121]]

皆等分以布灑星日，一處擣篩，取其香末， ^1-5121

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5122]]

當以此呪呪一百八遍。 ^1-5122

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5123]]

呪曰： ^1-5123

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5124]]

「怛姪他 蘇訖栗帝 訖栗帝訖栗帝劫摩怛里 繕怒羯囉滯 郝羯喇滯 因達囉闍利膩 鑠羯㘓滯 鉢設姪囇 阿伐底羯細 計娜矩覩矩覩 脚迦鼻囇 劫鼻囇劫鼻囇劫毘囉末底(丁里反) 尸羅末底那底度囉末底哩 波伐雉畔稚囇 室囇室囇薩底悉體羝莎訶 ^1-5124

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5125]]

「若樂如法洗浴時， ^1-5125

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5126]]

應作壇場方八肘； ^1-5126

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5127]]

可於寂靜安隱處， ^1-5127

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5128]]

念所求事不離心。 ^1-5128

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5129]]

應塗牛糞作其壇， ^1-5129

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5130]]

於上普散諸花彩； ^1-5130

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5131]]

當以淨潔金銀器， ^1-5131

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5132]]

盛滿美味并乳蜜。 ^1-5132

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5133]]

於彼壇場四門所， ^1-5133

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5134]]

四人守護法如常； ^1-5134

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5135]]

令四童子好嚴身， ^1-5135

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5136]]

各於一角持瓶水。 ^1-5136

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5137]]

於此常燒安息香， ^1-5137

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5138]]

五音之樂聲不絕； ^1-5138

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5139]]

幡蓋莊嚴懸繒綵， ^1-5139

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5140]]

安在壇場之四邊。 ^1-5140

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5141]]

復於場內置明鏡， ^1-5141

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5142]]

利刀兼箭各四枚； ^1-5142

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5143]]

於壇中心埋大盆， ^1-5143

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5144]]

應以漏版安其上。 ^1-5144

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5145]]

用前香末以和湯， ^1-5145

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5146]]

亦復安在於壇內； ^1-5146

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5147]]

既作如斯布置已， ^1-5147

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5148]]

然後誦呪結其壇。 ^1-5148

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5149]]

「結界呪曰： ^1-5149

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5150]]

「怛姪他頞喇計娜也泥(去)呬囇弭囇祇囇企企囇莎訶 ^1-5150

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5151]]

「如是結界已， ^1-5151

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5152]]

方入於壇內； ^1-5152

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5153]]

呪水三七遍， ^1-5153

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5154]]

散灑於四方。 ^1-5154

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5155]]

次可呪香湯， ^1-5155

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5156]]

滿一百八遍； ^1-5156

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5157]]

四邊安幔障， ^1-5157

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5158]]

然後洗浴身。 ^1-5158

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5159]]

「呪水呪湯呪曰： ^1-5159

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5160]]

「怛姪他(一)索揭智(貞勵反，下同)(二)毘揭智(三)毘揭荼伐底(四)莎訶(五) ^1-5160

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5161]]

「若洗浴訖，其洗浴湯及壇場中供養飲食棄河池內，餘皆收攝。 ^1-5161

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5162]]

如是浴已，方著淨衣， ^1-5162

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5163]]

既出壇場，入淨室內。 ^1-5163

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5164]]

呪師教其發弘誓願， ^1-5164

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5165]]

永斷眾惡， ^1-5165

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5166]]

常修諸善， ^1-5166

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5167]]

於諸有情興大悲心， ^1-5167

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5168]]

以是因緣當獲無量隨心福報。」 ^1-5168

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5169]]

復說頌曰： ^1-5169

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5170]]

「若有病苦諸眾生， ^1-5170

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5171]]

種種方藥治不差； ^1-5171

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5172]]

若依如是洗浴法， ^1-5172

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5173]]

并復讀誦斯經典。 ^1-5173

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5174]]

常於日夜念不散， ^1-5174

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5175]]

專想慇懃生信心； ^1-5175

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5176]]

所有患苦盡消除， ^1-5176

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5177]]

解脫貧窮足財寶。 ^1-5177

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5178]]

四方星辰及日月， ^1-5178

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5179]]

威神擁護得延年； ^1-5179

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5180]]

吉祥安隱福德增， ^1-5180

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5181]]

災變厄難皆除遣。 ^1-5181

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5182]]

「次誦護身呪三七遍。 ^1-5182

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5183]]

呪曰： ^1-5183

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5184]]

「怛姪他三謎毘三謎莎訶索揭滯毘揭滯莎訶毘揭茶(亭耶反)伐底莎訶娑揭囉三步多也莎訶塞建陀摩多也莎訶尼攞建佗也莎訶阿鉢囉市哆毘𠼝耶也莎訶呬摩槃哆三步多也莎訶阿儞蜜攞薄怛囉也莎訶南謨薄伽伐都跋囉甜摩寫莎訶南謨薩囉酸(蘇活)底莫訶提鼻裔莎訶悉甸覩漫(此云成就我某甲)曼怛囉鉢拖莎訶怛喇覩仳姪哆_跋囉甜摩奴末覩莎訶」 ^1-5184

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5185]]

爾時， ^1-5185

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5186]]

大辯才天女說洗浴法壇場呪已， ^1-5186

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5187]]

前禮佛足， ^1-5187

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5188]]

白佛言： ^1-5188

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5189]]

「世尊！ ^1-5189

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5190]]

若有苾芻、 ^1-5190

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5191]]

苾芻尼、 ^1-5191

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5192]]

鄔波索迦、 ^1-5192

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5193]]

鄔波斯迦， ^1-5193

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5194]]

受持讀誦、書寫流布是妙經王， ^1-5194

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5195]]

如說行者， ^1-5195

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5196]]

若在城邑聚落、曠野山林、僧尼住處， ^1-5196

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5197]]

我為是人將諸眷屬作天伎樂， ^1-5197

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5198]]

來詣其所而為擁護， ^1-5198

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5199]]

除諸病苦、 ^1-5199

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5200]]

流星變怪、 ^1-5200

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5201]]

疫疾鬪諍、 ^1-5201

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5202]]

王法所拘、 ^1-5202

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5203]]

惡夢惡神為障礙者，蠱道厭術悉皆除殄， ^1-5203

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5204]]

饒益是等持經之人。 ^1-5204

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5205]]

苾芻等眾及諸聽者，皆令速渡生死大海，不退菩提。」 ^1-5205

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5206]]

爾時，世尊聞是說已，讚辯才天女言： ^1-5206

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5207]]

「善哉！善哉！天女！汝能安樂利益無量無邊有情，說此神呪及以香水壇場法式，果報難思。 ^1-5207

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5208]]

汝當擁護最勝經王，勿令隱沒，常得流通。」 ^1-5208

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5209]]

爾時，大辯才天女禮佛足已， ^1-5209

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5210]]

還復本座。 ^1-5210

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5211]]

爾時，法師授記憍陳如婆羅門，承佛威力，於大眾前讚請辯才天女曰： ^1-5211

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5212]]

「聰明勇進辯才天， ^1-5212

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5213]]

人天供養悉應受； ^1-5213

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5214]]

名聞世間遍充滿， ^1-5214

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5215]]

能與一切眾生願。 ^1-5215

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5216]]

依高山頂勝住處， ^1-5216

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5217]]

葺茅為室在中居； ^1-5217

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5218]]

恒結軟草以為衣， ^1-5218

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5219]]

在處常翹於一足。 ^1-5219

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5220]]

諸天大眾皆來集， ^1-5220

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5221]]

咸同一心申讚請； ^1-5221

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5222]]

惟願智慧辯才天， ^1-5222

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5223]]

以妙言詞施一切。」 ^1-5223

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5224]]

爾時，辯才天女即便受請，為說呪曰： ^1-5224

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5225]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5259]]

天眼他心皆悉得。」 ^1-5309

勇猛常行大精進。 ^1-5319

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5323]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5334]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5335]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5340]]

或執三戟頭圓髻， ^1-5340

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5341]]

左右恒持日月旗； ^1-5341

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5342]]

黑月九日十一日， ^1-5342

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5343]]

於此時中當供養。 ^1-5343

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5344]]

或現婆蘇大天女， ^1-5344

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5345]]

見有鬪戰心常愍； ^1-5345

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5346]]

觀察一切有情中， ^1-5346

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5347]]

天女最勝無過者。 ^1-5347

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5348]]

權現牧牛歡喜女， ^1-5348

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5349]]

與天戰時常得勝； ^1-5349

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5350]]

能久安住於世間， ^1-5350

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5351]]

亦為和忍及暴惡。 ^1-5351

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5352]]

大婆羅門四明法， ^1-5352

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5353]]

幻化呪等悉皆通； ^1-5353

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5354]]

於天仙中得自在， ^1-5354

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5355]]

能為種子及大地。 ^1-5355

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5356]]

諸天女等集會時， ^1-5356

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5357]]

如大海潮必來應； ^1-5357

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5358]]

於諸龍神藥叉眾， ^1-5358

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5359]]

咸為上首能調伏。 ^1-5359

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5360]]

於諸女中最梵行， ^1-5360

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5361]]

出言猶如世間主； ^1-5361

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5362]]

於王住處如蓮華， ^1-5362

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5363]]

若在河津喻橋栰。 ^1-5363

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5364]]

面貌猶如盛滿月， ^1-5364

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5365]]

具足多聞作依處； ^1-5365

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5366]]

辯才勝出若高峯， ^1-5366

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5367]]

念者皆與為洲渚。 ^1-5367

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5368]]

阿蘇羅等諸天眾， ^1-5368

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5369]]

咸共稱讚其功德； ^1-5369

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5370]]

乃至千眼帝釋主， ^1-5370

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5371]]

以殷重心而觀察。 ^1-5371

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5372]]

眾生若有悕求事， ^1-5372

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5373]]

悉能令彼速得成； ^1-5373

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5374]]

亦令聰辯具聞持， ^1-5374

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5375]]

於大地中為第一。 ^1-5375

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5376]]

於此十方世界中， ^1-5376

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5377]]

如大燈明常普照； ^1-5377

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5378]]

乃至神鬼諸禽獸， ^1-5378

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5379]]

咸皆遂彼所求心。 ^1-5379

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5380]]

於諸女中若山峯， ^1-5380

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5381]]

同昔仙人久住世； ^1-5381

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5382]]

如少女天常離欲， ^1-5382

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5383]]

實語猶如大世主。 ^1-5383

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5384]]

普見世間差別類， ^1-5384

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5385]]

乃至欲界諸天宮； ^1-5385

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5386]]

唯有天女獨稱尊， ^1-5386

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5387]]

不見有情能勝者。 ^1-5387

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5388]]

若於戰陣恐怖處， ^1-5388

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5389]]

或見墮在火坑中； ^1-5389

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5390]]

河津險難賊盜時， ^1-5390

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5391]]

悉能令彼除怖畏。 ^1-5391

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5392]]

或被王法所枷縛， ^1-5392

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5393]]

或為怨讎行殺害； ^1-5393

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5394]]

若能專注心不移， ^1-5394

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5395]]

決定解脫諸憂苦。 ^1-5395

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5396]]

於善惡人皆擁護， ^1-5396

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5397]]

慈悲愍念常現前； ^1-5397

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5398]]

是故我以至誠心， ^1-5398

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5399]]

稽首歸依大天女。」 ^1-5399

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5400]]

爾時，婆羅門復以呪讚天女曰： ^1-5400

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5401]]

「敬禮敬禮世間尊， ^1-5401

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5402]]

於諸母中最為勝； ^1-5402

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5403]]

三種世間咸供養， ^1-5403

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5404]]

面貌容儀人樂觀。 ^1-5404

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5405]]

種種妙德以嚴身， ^1-5405

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5406]]

目如脩廣青蓮葉； ^1-5406

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5407]]

福智光明名稱滿， ^1-5407

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5408]]

譬如無價末尼珠。 ^1-5408

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5409]]

我今讚歎最勝者， ^1-5409

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5410]]

悉能成辦所求心； ^1-5410

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5411]]

真實功德妙吉祥， ^1-5411

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5412]]

譬如蓮花極清淨。 ^1-5412

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5413]]

身色端嚴皆樂見， ^1-5413

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5414]]

眾相希有不思議； ^1-5414

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5415]]

能放無垢智光明， ^1-5415

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5416]]

於諸念中為最勝。 ^1-5416

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5417]]

猶如師子獸中上， ^1-5417

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5418]]

常以八臂自莊嚴； ^1-5418

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5419]]

各持弓箭刀矟斧， ^1-5419

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5420]]

長杵鐵輪并羂索。 ^1-5420

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5421]]

端正樂見如滿月， ^1-5421

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5422]]

言詞無滯出和音； ^1-5422

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5423]]

若有眾生心願求， ^1-5423

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5424]]

善事隨念令圓滿。 ^1-5424

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5425]]

帝釋諸天咸供養， ^1-5425

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5426]]

皆共稱讚可歸依； ^1-5426

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5427]]

眾德能生不思議， ^1-5427

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5428]]

一切時中起恭敬。 「莎訶(此上呪頌是呪亦是讚，若持呪時必先誦之) ^1-5428

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5429]]

「若欲祈請辯才天， ^1-5429

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5430]]

依此呪讚言詞句； ^1-5430

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5431]]

晨朝清淨至誠誦， ^1-5431

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5432]]

於所求事悉隨心。」 ^1-5432

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5433]]

爾時，佛告婆羅門：「善哉！善哉！汝能如是利益眾生，施與安樂，讚彼天女，請求加護，獲福無邊。」 ^1-5433

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5434]]

(此品呪法有略有廣，或開或合前後不同，梵本既多，但依一譯，後勘者知之) ^1-5434

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5435]]

金光明最勝王經卷第八大唐三藏沙門義淨奉制譯 ^1-5435

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5436]]

大辯才天女品第十五之二 ^1-5436

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5437]]

爾時，憍陳如婆羅門說上讚歎及呪讚法，讚辯才天女已， ^1-5437

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5438]]

告諸大眾： ^1-5438

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5439]]

「仁等！若欲請辯才天女哀愍加護，於現世中得無礙辯，聰明大智， ^1-5439

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5440]]

巧妙言詞， ^1-5440

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5441]]

博綜奇才， ^1-5441

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5442]]

論議文飾， ^1-5442

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5443]]

隨意成就無疑滯者， ^1-5443

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5444]]

應當如是至誠殷重而請召言： ^1-5444

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5445]]

「南謨佛陀也！ ^1-5445

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5446]]

南謨達摩也！ ^1-5446

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5447]]

南謨僧伽也！ ^1-5447

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5448]]

南謨諸菩薩眾、獨覺、聲聞一切賢聖！ ^1-5448

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5449]]

過去現在十方諸佛，悉皆已習真實之語， ^1-5449

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5450]]

能隨順說當機實語， ^1-5450

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5451]]

無虛誑語， ^1-5451

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5452]]

已於無量俱胝大劫常說實語，有實語者悉皆隨喜； ^1-5452

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5453]]

以不妄語故， ^1-5453

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5454]]

出廣長舌能覆於面， ^1-5454

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5455]]

覆贍部洲及四天下，能覆一千、二千、三千世界，普覆十方世界，圓滿周遍，不可思議， ^1-5455

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5456]]

能除一切煩惱炎熱。 敬禮敬禮一切諸佛如是舌相， ^1-5456

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5457]]

願我某甲皆得成就微妙辯才，至心歸命。 ^1-5457

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5458]]

「敬禮諸佛妙辯才， ^1-5458

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5459]]

諸大菩薩妙辯才， ^1-5459

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5460]]

獨覺聖者妙辯才， ^1-5460

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5461]]

四向四果妙辯才， ^1-5461

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5462]]

四聖諦語妙辯才， ^1-5462

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5463]]

正行正見妙辯才， ^1-5463

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5464]]

梵眾諸仙妙辯才， ^1-5464

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5465]]

大天烏摩妙辯才， ^1-5465

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5466]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5498]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5505]]

我說無誑語； ^1-5505

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5506]]

天女妙辯才， ^1-5506

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5507]]

令我得成就。 ^1-5507

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5508]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5509]]

令我語無滯； ^1-5509

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5510]]

速入身口內， ^1-5510

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5511]]

聰明足辯才。 ^1-5511

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5512]]

調伏諸眾生。 ^1-5515

我所出語時， ^1-5516

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5517]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5520]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5521]]

事不成就者； ^1-5521

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5522]]

天女之實語， ^1-5522

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5523]]

皆悉成虛妄。 ^1-5523

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5524]]

有作無間罪， ^1-5524

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5525]]

佛語令調伏； ^1-5525

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5526]]

及以阿羅漢， ^1-5526

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5527]]

所有報恩語。 ^1-5527

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5528]]

舍利子目連， ^1-5528

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5529]]

世尊眾第一； ^1-5529

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5530]]

斯等真實語， ^1-5530

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5531]]

願我皆成就。 ^1-5531

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5532]]

我今皆召請，佛之聲聞眾， ^1-5532

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5533]]

皆願速來至， ^1-5533

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5534]]

成就我求心， ^1-5534

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5535]]

所求真實語， ^1-5535

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5536]]

皆願無虛誑。 ^1-5536

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5537]]

上從色究竟， ^1-5537

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5538]]

及以淨居天， ^1-5538

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5539]]

大梵及梵輔， ^1-5539

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5540]]

一切梵王眾， ^1-5540

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5541]]

索訶世界主， ^1-5542

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5543]]

并及諸眷屬， ^1-5543

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5544]]

我今皆請召； ^1-5544

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5545]]

惟願降慈悲， ^1-5545

哀憐同攝受。 ^1-5546

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5547]]

他化自在天， ^1-5547

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5548]]

及以樂變化； ^1-5548

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5549]]

覩史多天眾， ^1-5549

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5550]]

慈氏當成佛。 ^1-5550

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5551]]

夜摩諸天眾， ^1-5551

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5552]]

及三十三天； ^1-5552

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5553]]

四大王眾天， ^1-5553

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5554]]

一切諸天眾。 ^1-5554

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5555]]

地水火風神， ^1-5555

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5556]]

依妙高山住； ^1-5556

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5557]]

七海山神眾， ^1-5557

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5558]]

所有諸眷屬。 ^1-5558

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5559]]

滿財及五頂， ^1-5559

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5560]]

日月諸星辰； ^1-5560

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5561]]

如是諸天眾， ^1-5561

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5562]]

令世間安隱。 ^1-5562

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5563]]

斯等諸天神， ^1-5563

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5564]]

不樂作罪業； ^1-5564

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5565]]

敬禮鬼子母， ^1-5565

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5566]]

及最小愛兒。 ^1-5566

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5567]]

天龍藥叉眾， ^1-5567

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5568]]

乾闥阿蘇羅， ^1-5568

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5569]]

及以緊那羅， ^1-5569

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5570]]

莫呼洛伽等。 ^1-5570

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5571]]

我以世尊力， ^1-5571

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5572]]

悉皆申請召； ^1-5572

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5573]]

願降慈悲心， ^1-5573

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5574]]

與我無礙辯。 ^1-5574

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5575]]

一切人天眾， ^1-5575

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5576]]

能了他心者； ^1-5576

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5577]]

皆願加神力， ^1-5577

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5578]]

與我妙辯才。 ^1-5578

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5579]]

乃至盡虛空， ^1-5579

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5580]]

周遍於法界； ^1-5580

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5581]]

所有含生類， ^1-5581

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5582]]

與我妙辯才。」 ^1-5582

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5583]]

爾時，辯才天女聞是請已， ^1-5583

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5584]]

告婆羅門言： ^1-5584

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5585]]

「善哉！大士！ ^1-5585

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5586]]

若有男子女人能依如是呪及呪讚， ^1-5586

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5587]]

如前所說受持法式， ^1-5587

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5588]]

歸敬三寶， ^1-5588

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5589]]

虔心正念， ^1-5589

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5590]]

於所求事， ^1-5590

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5591]]

皆不唐捐， ^1-5591

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5592]]

兼復受持讀誦此《金光明》微妙經典， ^1-5592

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5593]]

所願求者，無不果遂， ^1-5593

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5594]]

速得成就， ^1-5594

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5595]]

除不至心。」 ^1-5595

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5596]]

時婆羅門，深心歡喜，合掌頂受。 ^1-5596

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5597]]

爾時，佛告辯才天女： ^1-5597

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5598]]

「善哉！善哉！善女天！ ^1-5598

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5599]]

汝能流布是妙經王， ^1-5599

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5600]]

擁護所有受持經者， ^1-5600

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-5601]]

及能利益一切眾生，令得安樂，說如是法， ^1-5601

 ^1-6153

『有王法正論， ^1-6182

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-6184]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-6185]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-6186]]

我依此論， ^1-6187

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-6188]]

於二萬歲善治國土， ^1-6188

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-6189]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-6190]]

勿以非法而治於國。 ^1-6191

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-6192]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-6194]]

當為汝說。 』 ^1-6194

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-6204]]

集在金剛山； ^1-6206

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-6207]]

四王從座起， ^1-6207

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-6208]]

請問於大梵： ^1-6208

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-6209]]

「梵主最勝尊， ^1-6209

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-6210]]

天中大自在； ^1-6210

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-6211]]

願哀愍我等， ^1-6211

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-6212]]

為斷諸疑惑。 ^1-6212

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-6213]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-6220]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-6221]]

_如是護世間， ^1-6221

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-6222]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-6223]]

_爾時梵天主， ^1-6223

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-6224]]

即便為彼說：_ ^1-6224

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-6225]]

「護世汝當知， ^1-6225

我說應善聽。 ^1-6228

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-6229]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-6230]]

生天得作王； ^1-6230

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-6231]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-6232]]

統領為人主。 ^1-6232

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-6233]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-6234]]

然後入母胎； ^1-6234

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-6235]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-6236]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-6237]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-6238]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-6239]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-6240]]

亦得名天子。 ^1-6240

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-6241]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-6242]]

分力助人王； ^1-6242

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-6243]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-6244]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-6245]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-6246]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-6247]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-6248]]

使得生天上。 ^1-6248

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-6249]]

_人及蘇羅眾， ^1-6249

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-6250]]

并揵闥婆等； ^1-6250

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-6251]]

_羅剎栴茶羅， ^1-6251

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-6252]]

悉皆資半力。 ^1-6252

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-6253]]

_父母資半力， ^1-6253

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-6254]]

令捨惡修善； ^1-6254

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-6255]]

 ^1-6632

{} ^1-6634

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-6635]]

爾時，世尊告大吉祥天女曰： ^1-6635

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-6636]]

「若有淨信善男子、善女人，欲於過去、未來、現在諸佛，以不可思議廣大微妙供養之具而為奉獻， ^1-6636

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-6637]]

及欲解了三世諸佛甚深行處， ^1-6637

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-6638]]

是人應當決定至心，隨是經王所在之處，城邑聚落或山澤中， ^1-6638

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-6639]]

廣為眾生敷演流布； ^1-6639

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-6641]]

攝耳用心。 」 ^1-6641

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-6642]]

於時世尊，即為彼天及諸大眾說伽他曰： ^1-6642

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-6643]]

_「若欲於諸佛， ^1-6643

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-6646]]

甚深境界者。 ^1-6646

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-6647]]

至其所住處。 ^1-6649

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-6650]]

_此經難思議， ^1-6650

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-6651]]

能生諸功德； ^1-6651

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-6652]]

_無邊大苦海， ^1-6652

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-6653]]

解脫諸有情。 ^1-6653

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-6654]]

_我觀此經王， ^1-6654

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-6655]]

初中後皆善； ^1-6655

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-6656]]

_甚深不可測， ^1-6656

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-6657]]

佛告樹神： ^1-7034

方得授記。 ^1-7038

 ^1-7050

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7058]]

善女天！過去無量不可思議阿僧企耶劫， ^1-7058

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7059]]

爾時有佛出現於世，名曰寶髻如來、應、正遍知、明行足、善逝、世間解、無上士、調御丈夫、天人師、佛、世尊。 ^1-7059

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7060]]

善女天！時彼世尊般涅槃後， ^1-7060

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7061]]

正法滅已， ^1-7061

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7062]]

於像法中，有王名曰天自在光， ^1-7062

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7063]]

常以正法化於人民，猶如父母。 ^1-7063

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7064]]

是王國中，有一長者名曰持水， ^1-7064

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7065]]

善解醫明， ^1-7065

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7066]]

妙通八術， ^1-7066

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7067]]

眾生病苦，四大不調，咸能救療。 ^1-7067

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7068]]

善女天！爾時持水長者，唯有一子，名曰流水， ^1-7068

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7069]]

顏容端正， ^1-7069

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7070]]

人所樂觀， ^1-7070

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7071]]

受性聰敏， ^1-7071

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7072]]

妙閑諸論， ^1-7072

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7073]]

書畫算印，無不通達。 ^1-7073

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7074]]

時王國內有無量百千諸眾生類， ^1-7074

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7075]]

皆遇疫疾，眾苦所逼， ^1-7075

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7076]]

乃至無有歡樂之心。 ^1-7076

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7077]]

善女天！爾時長者子流水，見是無量百千眾生受諸病苦， ^1-7077

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7078]]

起大悲心， ^1-7078

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7079]]

作如是念： ^1-7079

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7080]]

『無量眾生為諸極苦之所逼迫， ^1-7080

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7081]]

我父長者，雖善醫方，妙通八術， ^1-7081

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7082]]

能療眾病，四大增損， ^1-7082

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7083]]

然已衰邁，老耄虛羸， ^1-7083

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7084]]

要假扶策，方能進步， ^1-7084

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7085]]

不復能往城邑聚落救諸病苦。 ^1-7085

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7086]]

今有無量百千眾生，皆遇重病， ^1-7086

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7087]]

無能救者， ^1-7087

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7088]]

我今當至大醫父所， ^1-7088

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7089]]

諮問治病醫方祕法， ^1-7089

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7090]]

若得解已， ^1-7090

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7091]]

當往城邑聚落之所， ^1-7091

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7092]]

救諸眾生種種疾病，令於長夜得受安樂。』 ^1-7092

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7093]]

時長者子作是念已， ^1-7093

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7094]]

即詣父所， ^1-7094

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7095]]

稽首禮足， ^1-7095

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7096]]

合掌恭敬， ^1-7096

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7097]]

却住一面， ^1-7097

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7098]]

即以伽他，請其父曰： ^1-7098

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7099]]

「『慈父當哀愍，我欲救眾生；今請諸醫方，幸願為我說。 ^1-7099

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7100]]

云何身衰壞， ^1-7100

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7101]]

諸大有增損？ ^1-7101

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7102]]

復在何時中， ^1-7102

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7103]]

能生諸疾病？ ^1-7103

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7104]]

云何噉飲食， ^1-7104

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7105]]

得受於安樂， ^1-7105

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7106]]

能使內身中，火熱不衰損？ ^1-7106

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7107]]

眾生有四病，風黃熱痰癊；及以總集病， ^1-7107

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7108]]

云何而療治？ ^1-7108

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7109]]

何時風病起？ ^1-7109

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7110]]

何時熱病發？ ^1-7110

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7111]]

何時動痰癊？ ^1-7111

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7112]]

何時總集生？』 ^1-7112

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7113]]

時彼長者聞子請已，復以伽他而答之曰： ^1-7113

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7114]]

「『我今依古仙，所有療病法； ^1-7114

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7115]]

次第為汝說，善聽救眾生。 ^1-7115

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7116]]

三月是春時， ^1-7116

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7117]]

三月名為夏， ^1-7117

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7118]]

三月名秋分， ^1-7118

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7119]]

三月謂冬時。 ^1-7119

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7120]]

此據一年中，三三而別說； ^1-7120

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7121]]

二二為一節，便成歲六時。 ^1-7121

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7122]]

初二是花時， ^1-7122

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7123]]

三四名熱際； ^1-7123

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7124]]

五六名雨際， ^1-7124

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7125]]

七八謂秋時； ^1-7125

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7126]]

九十是寒時， ^1-7126

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7127]]

後二名氷雪； ^1-7127

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7128]]

既知如是別， ^1-7128

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7129]]

授藥勿令差。 ^1-7129

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7130]]

當隨此時中，調息於飲食； ^1-7130

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7131]]

入腹令消散，眾病則不生。 ^1-7131

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7132]]

節氣若變改，四大有推移； ^1-7132

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7133]]

此時無藥資，必生於病苦。 ^1-7133

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7134]]

醫人解四時，復知其六節； ^1-7134

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7135]]

明閑身七界，食藥使無差。 ^1-7135

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7136]]

謂味界血肉，膏骨及髓腦； ^1-7136

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7137]]

病入此中時，知其可療不？ ^1-7137

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7138]]

病有四種別， ^1-7138

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7139]]

謂風熱痰癊；及以總集病， ^1-7139

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7140]]

應知發動時。 ^1-7140

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7141]]

春中痰癊動， ^1-7141

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7142]]

夏內風病生； ^1-7142

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7143]]

秋時黃熱增， ^1-7143

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7144]]

冬節三俱起。 ^1-7144

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7145]]

春食澁熱辛， ^1-7145

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7146]]

夏膩熱醎醋； ^1-7146

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7147]]

秋時冷甜膩， ^1-7147

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7148]]

冬酸澁膩甜。 ^1-7148

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7149]]

於此四時中，服藥及飲食； ^1-7149

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7150]]

若依如是味， ^1-7150

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7151]]

眾病無由生。 ^1-7151

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7152]]

食後病由癊，食消時由熱； ^1-7152

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7153]]

消後起由風， ^1-7153

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7154]]

准時須識病。 ^1-7154

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7155]]

既識病源已， ^1-7155

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7156]]

隨病而設藥； ^1-7156

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7157]]

假令患狀殊， ^1-7157

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7158]]

先須療其本。 ^1-7158

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7159]]

風病服油膩， ^1-7159

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7160]]

患熱利為良； ^1-7160

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7161]]

癊病應變吐， ^1-7161

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7162]]

總集須三藥。 ^1-7162

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7163]]

風熱癊俱有，是名為總集； ^1-7163

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7164]]

雖知病起時， ^1-7164

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7165]]

應觀其本性。 ^1-7165

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7166]]

如是觀知已， ^1-7166

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7167]]

順時而授藥； ^1-7167

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7168]]

飲食藥無差，斯名善醫者。 ^1-7168

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7169]]

復應知八術，總攝諸醫方； ^1-7169

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7170]]

於此若明閑， ^1-7170

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7171]]

可療眾生病。 ^1-7171

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7172]]

謂針刺傷破，身疾并鬼神； ^1-7172

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7173]]

惡毒及孩童，延年增氣力。 ^1-7173

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7174]]

先觀彼形色，語言及性行； ^1-7174

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7175]]

然後問其夢， ^1-7175

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7176]]

知風熱癊殊。 ^1-7176

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7177]]

乾瘦少頭髮，其心無定住； ^1-7177

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7178]]

多語夢飛行，斯人是風性。 ^1-7178

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7179]]

少年生白髮， ^1-7179

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7180]]

多汗及多瞋； ^1-7180

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7181]]

聰明夢見火， ^1-7181

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7182]]

斯人是熱性。 ^1-7182

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7183]]

心定身平整， ^1-7183

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7184]]

慮審頭津膩； ^1-7184

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7185]]

夢見水白物， ^1-7185

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7186]]

是癊性應知。 ^1-7186

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7187]]

總集性俱有，或二或具三； ^1-7187

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7188]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7198]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7199]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7200]]

下脣垂向下。 ^1-7200

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7201]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7202]]

能除一切病， ^1-7202

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7203]]

無忌藥中王。 ^1-7203

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7204]]

又三果三辛， ^1-7204

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7205]]

諸藥中易得； ^1-7205

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7206]]

沙糖蜜蘇乳， ^1-7206

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7207]]

此能療眾病。 ^1-7207

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7208]]

自餘諸藥物， ^1-7208

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7209]]

隨病可增加； ^1-7209

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7210]]

先起慈愍心， ^1-7210

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7211]]

莫規於財利。 ^1-7211

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7212]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7213]]

以此救眾生， ^1-7213

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7214]]

當獲無邊果。』 ^1-7214

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7215]]

「善女天！爾時，長者子流水親問其父八術之要， ^1-7215

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7216]]

四大增損， ^1-7216

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7217]]

時節不同， ^1-7217

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7218]]

餌藥方法，既善了知， ^1-7218

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7219]]

自忖堪能救療眾病， ^1-7219

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7220]]

即便遍至城邑聚落所在之處， ^1-7220

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7221]]

隨有百千萬億病苦眾生，皆至其所， ^1-7221

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7222]]

善言慰喻， ^1-7222

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7223]]

作如是語： ^1-7223

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7224]]

『我是醫人！我是醫人！ ^1-7224

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7225]]

善知方藥， ^1-7225

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7226]]

今為汝等療治眾病， ^1-7226

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7227]]

悉令除愈。』 ^1-7227

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7228]]

善女天！爾時眾人聞長者子善言慰喻，許為治病， ^1-7228

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7229]]

時有無量百千眾生遇極重病， ^1-7229

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7230]]

聞是語已， ^1-7230

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7231]]

身心踊躍，得未曾有。 ^1-7231

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7232]]

以此因緣，所有病苦悉得蠲除， ^1-7232

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7233]]

氣力充實，平復如本。 ^1-7233

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7234]]

善女天！爾時復有無量百千眾生，病苦深重難療治者， ^1-7234

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7235]]

即共往詣長者子所， ^1-7235

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7236]]

重請醫療。 ^1-7236

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7237]]

時長者子，即以妙藥令服 ^1-7237

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7238]]

皆蒙除差。 ^1-7238

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7239]]

善女天！是長者子於此國內，治百千萬億眾生病苦，悉得除差。 ^1-7239

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7240]]

 ^1-7240

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7241]]

金光明最勝王經 ^1-7241

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7242]]

長者子流水品第二十五 ^1-7242

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7243]]

爾時，佛告菩提樹神： ^1-7243

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7244]]

「善女天！爾時長者子流水，於往昔時，在天自在光王國內， ^1-7244

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7245]]

療諸眾生所有病苦， ^1-7245

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7246]]

令得平復， ^1-7246

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7247]]

受安隱樂。 ^1-7247

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7248]]

時諸眾生以病除故， ^1-7248

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7249]]

多修福業， ^1-7249

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7250]]

廣行惠施， ^1-7250

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7251]]

以自歡娛。 ^1-7251

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7252]]

即共往詣長者子所， ^1-7252

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7253]]

咸生尊敬， ^1-7253

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7254]]

作如是言：『善哉！善哉！大長者子！善能滋長福德之事， ^1-7254

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7255]]

增益我等 ^1-7255

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7256]]

安隱壽命， ^1-7256

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7257]]

仁今實是大力醫王慈悲菩薩， ^1-7257

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7258]]

妙閑醫藥， ^1-7258

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7259]]

善療眾生無量病苦。』 ^1-7259

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7260]]

如是稱歎周遍城邑。 ^1-7260

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7261]]

「善女天！時長者子妻名水肩藏，有其二子， ^1-7261

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7262]]

一名水滿； ^1-7262

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7263]]

二名水藏。 ^1-7263

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7264]]

是時流水將其二子， ^1-7264

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7265]]

漸次遊行城邑聚落， ^1-7265

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7266]]

過空澤中深險之處， ^1-7266

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7267]]

見諸禽獸豺狼狐玃鵰鷲之屬食血肉者， ^1-7267

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7268]]

皆悉奔飛一向而去。 ^1-7268

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7269]]

時長者子作如是念： ^1-7269

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7270]]

『此諸禽獸何因緣故， ^1-7270

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7271]]

一向飛走？ ^1-7271

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7272]]

我當隨後暫往觀之。』 ^1-7272

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7273]]

即便隨去， ^1-7273

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7274]]

見有大池，名曰野生， ^1-7274

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7275]]

其水將盡， ^1-7275

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7276]]

於此池中多有眾魚。 ^1-7276

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7277]]

流水見已， ^1-7277

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7278]]

生大悲心。 ^1-7278

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7279]]

時有樹神示現半身， ^1-7279

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7280]]

作如是語：『善哉！善哉！ ^1-7280

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7281]]

善男子！汝有實義名流水者， ^1-7281

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7282]]

可愍此魚，應與其水。 ^1-7282

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7283]]

有二因緣，名為流水： ^1-7283

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7284]]

一能流水， ^1-7284

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7285]]

二能與水。 ^1-7285

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7286]]

汝今應當隨名而作。』 ^1-7286

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7287]]

是時流水問樹神言： ^1-7287

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7288]]

『此魚頭數為有幾何？』 ^1-7288

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7289]]

樹神答曰：『數滿十千。』 ^1-7289

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7290]]

「善女天！時長者子聞是數已， ^1-7290

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7291]]

倍益悲心。 ^1-7291

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7292]]

時此大池為日所曝， ^1-7292

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7293]]

餘水無幾， ^1-7293

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7294]]

是十千魚將入死門， ^1-7294

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7295]]

旋身婉轉， ^1-7295

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7296]]

見是長者心有所悕， ^1-7296

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7297]]

隨逐瞻視，目未曾捨。 ^1-7297

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7298]]

時長者子，見是事已， ^1-7298

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7299]]

馳趣四方， ^1-7299

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7300]]

欲覓於水，竟不能得。 ^1-7300

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7301]]

復望一邊見有大樹， ^1-7301

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7302]]

即便昇上折取枝葉 ^1-7302

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7303]]

為作蔭涼。 ^1-7303

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7304]]

復更推求，是池中水從何處來？ ^1-7304

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7305]]

尋覓不已，見一大河，名曰水生， ^1-7305

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7306]]

時此河邊有諸漁人為取魚故， ^1-7306

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7307]]

於河上流懸險之處， ^1-7307

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7308]]

決棄其水，不令下過， ^1-7308

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7309]]

於所決處，卒難修補。 ^1-7309

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7310]]

便作是念：『此崖深峻， ^1-7310

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7311]]

設百千人，時經三月亦未能斷， ^1-7311

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7312]]

況我一身而堪濟辦？』 ^1-7312

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7313]]

時長者子速還本城， ^1-7313

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7314]]

至大王所， ^1-7314

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7315]]

頭面禮足， ^1-7315

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7316]]

却住一面， ^1-7316

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7317]]

合掌恭敬 ^1-7317

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7318]]

作如是言：『我為大王國土人民，治種種病， ^1-7318

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7319]]

悉令安隱， ^1-7319

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7320]]

漸次遊行至其空澤， ^1-7320

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7321]]

見有一池，名曰野生，其水欲涸， ^1-7321

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7322]]

有十千魚為日所曝， ^1-7322

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7323]]

將死不久， ^1-7323

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7324]]

惟願大王慈悲愍念， ^1-7324

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7325]]

與二十大象暫往負水， ^1-7325

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7326]]

濟彼魚命，如我與諸病人壽命。』 ^1-7326

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7327]]

爾時大王即勅大臣，速疾與此醫王大象。 ^1-7327

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7328]]

時彼大臣，奉王勅已， ^1-7328

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7329]]

白長者子： ^1-7329

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7330]]

『善哉大士！ ^1-7330

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7331]]

仁今自可至象廐中， ^1-7331

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7332]]

隨意選取二十大象， ^1-7332

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7333]]

利益眾生， ^1-7333

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7334]]

令得安樂。』 ^1-7334

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7335]]

是時流水及其二子，將二十大象， ^1-7335

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7336]]

又從酒家多借皮囊， ^1-7336

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7337]]

往決水處， ^1-7337

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7338]]

以囊盛水， ^1-7338

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7339]]

象負至池， ^1-7339

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7340]]

瀉置池中， ^1-7340

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7341]]

水即彌滿，還復如故。 ^1-7341

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7342]]

「善女天！時長者子，於池四邊周旋而視， ^1-7342

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7343]]

時彼眾魚亦復隨逐循岸而行。 ^1-7343

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7344]]

時長者子復作是念： ^1-7344

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7345]]

『眾魚何故隨我而行？ ^1-7345

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7346]]

必為飢火之所惱逼， ^1-7346

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7347]]

復欲從我求索於食，我今當與。』 ^1-7347

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7348]]

爾時長者子流水告其子言： ^1-7348

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7349]]

『汝取一象最大力者， ^1-7349

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7350]]

速至家中啟父長者， ^1-7350

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7351]]

家中所有可食之物， ^1-7351

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7352]]

乃至父母食噉之分， ^1-7352

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7353]]

及以妻子奴婢之分， ^1-7353

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7354]]

悉皆收取，即可持來。』 ^1-7354

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7355]]

爾時二子受父教已， ^1-7355

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7356]]

乘最大象速往家中， ^1-7356

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7357]]

至祖父所， ^1-7357

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7358]]

說如上事。 ^1-7358

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7359]]

收取家中可食之物， ^1-7359

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7360]]

置於象上， ^1-7360

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7361]]

疾還父所，至彼池邊。 ^1-7361

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7362]]

是時流水見其子來， ^1-7362

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7363]]

身心喜躍， ^1-7363

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7364]]

遂取餅食遍散池中， ^1-7364

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7365]]

魚得食已，悉皆飽足。 ^1-7365

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7366]]

便作是念：『我今施食令魚得命， ^1-7366

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7367]]

願於來世當施法食充濟無邊。』 ^1-7367

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7368]]

復更思惟： ^1-7368

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7369]]

『我先曾於空閑林處，見一苾芻讀大乘經， ^1-7369

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7370]]

說十二緣生甚深法要。 ^1-7370

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7371]]

又經中說：「若有眾生臨命終時， ^1-7371

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7372]]

得聞寶髻如來名者， ^1-7372

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7373]]

即生天上。」 ^1-7373

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7374]]

我今當為是十千魚演說甚深十二緣起， ^1-7374

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7375]]

亦當稱說寶髻佛名。 ^1-7375

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7376]]

然贍部洲有二種人， ^1-7376

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7377]]

一者、深信大乘，二者、不信毀呰， ^1-7377

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7378]]

亦當為彼增長信心。』 ^1-7378

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7379]]

時長者子作如是念：『我入池中， ^1-7379

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7380]]

可為眾魚說深妙法。』 ^1-7380

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7381]]

作是念已，即便入水唱言： ^1-7381

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7382]]

『南謨過去寶髻如來、應、正遍知、明行足、善逝、世間解、無上士、調御丈夫、天人師、佛、世尊。 ^1-7382

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7383]]

此佛往昔修菩薩行時，作是誓願： ^1-7383

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7384]]

「於十方界所有眾生， ^1-7384

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7385]]

臨命終時聞我名者， ^1-7385

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7386]]

命終之後， ^1-7386

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7387]]

得生三十三天。」』 ^1-7387

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7388]]

爾時流水復為池魚演說如是甚深妙法： ^1-7388

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7389]]

『此有故彼有， ^1-7389

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7390]]

此生故彼生， ^1-7390

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7391]]

所謂無明緣行， ^1-7391

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7392]]

行緣識， ^1-7392

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7393]]

識緣名色， ^1-7393

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7394]]

名色緣六處， ^1-7394

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7395]]

六處緣觸， ^1-7395

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7396]]

觸緣受， ^1-7396

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7397]]

受緣愛， ^1-7397

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7398]]

愛緣取， ^1-7398

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7399]]

取緣有， ^1-7399

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7400]]

有緣生， ^1-7400

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7401]]

生緣老死憂悲苦惱。 ^1-7401

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7402]]

此滅故彼滅， ^1-7402

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7403]]

所謂無明滅則行滅， ^1-7403

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7404]]

行滅則識滅， ^1-7404

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7405]]

識滅則名色滅， ^1-7405
