![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7406]]

名色滅則六處滅， ^1-7406

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7407]]

六處滅則觸滅， ^1-7407

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7408]]

觸滅則受滅， ^1-7408

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7409]]

受滅則愛滅， ^1-7409

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7410]]

愛滅則取滅， ^1-7410

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7411]]

取滅則有滅， ^1-7411

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7412]]

有滅則生滅， ^1-7412

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7413]]

生滅則老死滅， ^1-7413

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7414]]

老死滅則憂悲苦惱滅， ^1-7414

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7415]]

如是純極苦蘊悉皆除滅。』 ^1-7415

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7416]]

說是法已， ^1-7416

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7417]]

復為宣說十二緣起相應陀羅尼曰： ^1-7417

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7418]]

「『怛姪他_毘折儞_毘折儞_毘折儞_僧塞枳儞_僧塞枳儞_僧塞枳儞_毘爾儞_毘爾儞_毘爾儞莎訶_怛姪他_那弭儞那弭儞_那弭儞_殺雉儞_殺雉儞_殺雉儞颯鉢哩設儞_颯鉢哩設儞_颯鉢哩設儞莎訶_怛姪他_薜達儞_薜達儞_薜達儞_窒里瑟儞儞_窒里瑟儞儞_窒里瑟儞儞鄔波地儞_鄔波地儞_鄔波地儞_莎訶_怛姪他_婆毘儞婆毘儞_婆毘儞_闍底儞_闍底儞_闍底儞_闍摩儞儞_闍摩儞儞_闍摩儞儞莎訶』」 ^1-7418

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7419]]

爾時，世尊為諸大眾說長者子昔緣之時， ^1-7419

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7420]]

諸人天眾歎未曾有。 ^1-7420

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7421]]

時四大天王各於其處， ^1-7421

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7422]]

異口同音作如是說： ^1-7422

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7423]]

「善哉釋迦尊，說妙法明呪；生福除眾惡，十二支相應。 ^1-7423

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7424]]

我等亦說呪，擁護如是法； ^1-7424

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7425]]

若有生違逆，不善隨順者。 ^1-7425

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7426]]

頭破作七分，猶如蘭香梢； ^1-7426

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7427]]

我等於佛前，共說其呪曰： ^1-7427

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7428]]

「怛姪他_呬里謎_揭睇健_陀哩_旃荼哩地囇_騷伐囇_石呬伐囇_補囉布囇矩矩末底_崎囉末底_達地目契_窶嚕婆_母嚕婆_具荼母嚕健提_杜嚕杜嚕_毘囇_醫泥悉悉泥沓(徒洽，下同)婫達沓婫_鄔悉怛哩_烏率吒囉伐底_頞剌娑伐底鉢杜摩伐底_俱蘇摩伐底_莎訶」 ^1-7428

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7429]]

佛告善女天： ^1-7429

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7430]]

「爾時長者子流水及其二子， ^1-7430

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7431]]

為彼池魚施水施食， ^1-7431

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7432]]

并說法已， ^1-7432

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7433]]

俱共還家。 ^1-7433

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7434]]

是長者子流水，復於後時因有聚會， ^1-7434

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7435]]

設眾伎樂，醉酒而臥。 ^1-7435

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7436]]

時十千魚同時命過， ^1-7436

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7437]]

生三十三天， ^1-7437

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7438]]

起如是念：『我等以何善業因緣生此天中？』 ^1-7438

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7439]]

便相謂曰：『我等先於贍部洲內， ^1-7439

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7440]]

墮傍生中，共受魚身， ^1-7440

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7441]]

長者子流水施我等水及以餅食， ^1-7441

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7442]]

復為我等說甚深法十二緣起及陀羅尼， ^1-7442

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7443]]

復稱寶髻如來名號，以是因緣，能令我等得生此天。 ^1-7443

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7444]]

是故我今咸應詣彼長者子所，報恩供養。』 ^1-7444

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7445]]

爾時十千天子即於天沒， ^1-7445

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7446]]

至贍部洲大醫王所。 ^1-7446

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7447]]

時長者子在高樓上安隱而睡， ^1-7447

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7448]]

時十千天子共以十千真珠瓔珞置其面邊， ^1-7448

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7449]]

復以十千置其足處， ^1-7449

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7450]]

復以十千置於右脇， ^1-7450

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7451]]

復以十千置左脇邊， ^1-7451

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7452]]

雨曼陀羅花、摩訶曼陀羅花， ^1-7452

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7453]]

積至于膝， ^1-7453

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7454]]

光明普照， ^1-7454

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7455]]

種種天樂出妙音聲， ^1-7455

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7456]]

令贍部洲有睡眠者皆悉覺悟， ^1-7456

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7457]]

長者子流水亦從睡寤。 ^1-7457

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7458]]

是時十千天子為供養已， ^1-7458

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7459]]

即於空中飛騰而去， ^1-7459

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7460]]

於天自在光王國內， ^1-7460

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7461]]

處處皆雨天妙蓮花， ^1-7461

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7462]]

是諸天子復至本處，空澤池中雨眾天花， ^1-7462

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7463]]

便於此沒，還天宮殿， ^1-7463

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7464]]

隨意自在，受五欲樂。 ^1-7464

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7465]]

「天自在光王至天曉已， ^1-7465

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7466]]

問諸大臣：『昨夜何緣忽現如是希有瑞相，放大光明？』 ^1-7466

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7467]]

大臣答言： ^1-7467

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7468]]

『大王當知！ ^1-7468

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7469]]

有諸天眾於長者子流水家中， ^1-7469

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7470]]

雨四十千真珠瓔珞及天曼陀羅花，積至于膝。』 ^1-7470

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7471]]

王告臣曰： ^1-7471

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7472]]

『詣長者家，喚取其子。』 ^1-7472

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7473]]

大臣受勅， ^1-7473

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7474]]

即至其家， ^1-7474

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7475]]

奉宣王命，喚長者子。 ^1-7475

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7476]]

時長者子即至王所。 ^1-7476

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7477]]

王曰：『何緣昨夜示現如是希有瑞相？』 ^1-7477

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7478]]

長者子言： ^1-7478

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7479]]

『如我思惟， ^1-7479

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7480]]

定應是彼池內眾魚，如經所說， ^1-7480

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7481]]

命終之後得生三十三天， ^1-7481

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7482]]

彼來報恩， ^1-7482

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7483]]

故現如是希奇之相。』 ^1-7483

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7484]]

王曰： ^1-7484

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7485]]

『何以得知？』 ^1-7485

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7486]]

流水答言： ^1-7486

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7487]]

『王可遣使，并我二子，往彼池所驗其虛實，彼十千魚為死為活？』 ^1-7487

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7488]]

王聞是語， ^1-7488

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7489]]

即便遣使及子向彼池邊， ^1-7489

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7490]]

見其池中多有曼陀羅花，積成大聚， ^1-7490

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7491]]

諸魚並死， ^1-7491

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7492]]

見已馳還為王廣說。 ^1-7492

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7493]]

王聞是已，心生歡喜， ^1-7493

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7494]]

歎未曾有。」 ^1-7494

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7495]]

爾時，佛告菩提樹神： ^1-7495

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7496]]

「善女天！汝今當知 ^1-7496

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7497]]

昔時長者子流水者，即我身是； ^1-7497

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7498]]

持水長者即妙幢是； ^1-7498

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7499]]

彼之二子，長子水滿即銀幢是， ^1-7499

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7500]]

次子水藏即銀光是； ^1-7500

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7501]]

彼天自在光王者，即汝菩提樹神是； ^1-7501

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7502]]

十千魚者即十千天子是。 ^1-7502

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7503]]

因我往昔以水濟魚，與食令飽， ^1-7503

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7504]]

為說甚深十二緣起，并此相應陀羅尼呪， ^1-7504

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7505]]

又為稱彼寶髻佛名， ^1-7505

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7506]]

因此善根得生天上， ^1-7506

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7507]]

今來我所， ^1-7507

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7508]]

歡喜聽法。 ^1-7508

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7509]]

我皆當為授於阿耨多羅三藐三菩提記， ^1-7509

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7510]]

說其名號。 ^1-7510

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7511]]

善女天！如我往昔於生死中，輪迴諸有，廣為利益， ^1-7511

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7512]]

今無量眾生悉令次第成無上覺，與其授記， ^1-7512

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7513]]

汝等皆應勤求出離， ^1-7513

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7514]]

勿為放逸。」 ^1-7514

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7515]]

爾時大眾聞說是已， ^1-7515

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7516]]

由大慈悲救護一切， ^1-7516

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7517]]

勤修苦行， ^1-7517

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7518]]

方能證獲無上菩提， ^1-7518

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7519]]

{悉皆悟解，} ^1-7519

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7520]]

咸發深心，信受歡喜。 ^1-7520

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7521]]

 ^1-7521

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7522]]

金光明最勝王經卷第十_大唐三藏沙門義淨奉_制譯 ^1-7522

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7523]]

捨身品第二十六 ^1-7523

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7524]]

 ^1-7524

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7525]]

爾時，世尊已為大眾說此十千天子往昔因緣， ^1-7525

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7526]]

復告菩提樹神及諸大眾： ^1-7526

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7527]]

「我於過去行菩薩道， ^1-7527

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7528]]

非但施水及食，濟彼魚命， ^1-7528

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7529]]

乃至亦捨所愛之身， ^1-7529

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7530]]

如是因緣，可共觀察。」 ^1-7530

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7531]]

爾時，如來、應、正等覺， ^1-7531

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7532]]

天上天下最勝最尊， ^1-7532

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7533]]

百千光明照十方界， ^1-7533

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7534]]

具一切智 ^1-7534

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7535]]

功德圓滿， ^1-7535

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7536]]

將諸苾芻及於大眾，至般遮羅聚落， ^1-7536

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7537]]

詣一林中。其地平正， ^1-7537

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7538]]

無諸荊棘， ^1-7538

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7539]]

名花軟草遍布其處。 ^1-7539

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7540]]

佛告具壽阿難陀： ^1-7540

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7541]]

「汝可於此樹下為我敷座。」 ^1-7541

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7542]]

時阿難陀受教 ^1-7542

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7543]]

敷已，白言：「世尊！其座敷訖，唯聖知時。」 ^1-7543

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7544]]

爾時世尊，即於座上加趺而坐，端身正念， ^1-7544

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7545]]

告諸苾芻： ^1-7545

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7546]]

「汝等樂欲見彼往昔苦行菩薩本舍利不？」 ^1-7546

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7547]]

諸苾芻言： ^1-7547

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7548]]

「我等樂見。」 ^1-7548

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7549]]

世尊即以百福莊嚴相好之手而按其地， ^1-7549

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7550]]

于時大地六種震動，即便開裂， ^1-7550

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7551]]

七寶制底忽然踊出，眾寶羅網莊嚴其上。 ^1-7551

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7552]]

大眾見已， ^1-7552

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7553]]

生希有心。 ^1-7553

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7554]]

爾時世尊即從座起， ^1-7554

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7555]]

作禮右遶， ^1-7555

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7556]]

還就本座， ^1-7556

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7557]]

告阿難陀： ^1-7557

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7558]]

「汝可開此制底之戶。」 ^1-7558

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7559]]

時阿難陀即開其戶， ^1-7559

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7560]]

見七寶函，奇珍間飾， ^1-7560

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7561]]

白言：「世尊！有七寶函，眾寶莊挍。」 ^1-7561

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7562]]

佛言：「汝可開函。」 ^1-7562

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7563]]

時阿難陀奉教開已， ^1-7563

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7564]]

見有舍利，白如珂雪拘物頭花， ^1-7564

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7565]]

即白佛言：「函有舍利，色妙異常。」 ^1-7565

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7566]]

佛言：「阿難陀！汝可持此大士骨來。」 ^1-7566

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7567]]

時阿難陀即取其骨， ^1-7567

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7568]]

奉授世尊。 ^1-7568

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7569]]

世尊受已， ^1-7569

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7570]]

告諸苾芻：「汝等應觀苦行菩薩遺身舍利。」 ^1-7570

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7571]]

而說頌曰： ^1-7571

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7572]]

「菩薩勝德相應慧， ^1-7572

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7573]]

勇猛精勤六度圓； ^1-7573

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7574]]

常修不息為菩提， ^1-7574

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7575]]

不捨堅固心無倦。」 ^1-7575

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7576]]

「汝等苾芻咸應禮敬菩薩本身， ^1-7576

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7577]]

此之舍利乃是無量戒、定、慧香之所熏馥， ^1-7577

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7578]]

最上福田，極難逢遇。」 ^1-7578

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7579]]

時諸苾芻及諸大眾，咸皆至心合掌， ^1-7579

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7580]]

恭敬頂禮舍利， ^1-7580

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7581]]

歎未曾有。 ^1-7581

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7582]]

時阿難陀前禮佛足， ^1-7582

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7583]]

白言：「世尊！如來大師出過一切， ^1-7583

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7584]]

為諸有情之所恭敬， ^1-7584

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7585]]

何因緣故，禮此身骨？」 ^1-7585

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7586]]

佛告阿難陀： ^1-7586

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7587]]

「我因此骨速得無上正等菩提， ^1-7587

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7588]]

為報往恩，我今致禮。」 ^1-7588

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7589]]

復告阿難陀： ^1-7589

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7590]]

「吾今為汝及諸大眾斷除疑惑， ^1-7590

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7591]]

說是舍利往昔因緣， ^1-7591

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7592]]

汝等善思， ^1-7592

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7593]]

當一心聽。」 ^1-7593

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7594]]

阿難陀曰：「我等樂聞， ^1-7594

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7595]]

願為開闡。」 ^1-7595

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7596]]

「阿難陀！過去世時有一國王，名曰大車， ^1-7596

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7597]]

巨富多財， ^1-7597

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7598]]

庫藏盈滿， ^1-7598

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7599]]

軍兵武勇， ^1-7599

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7600]]

眾所欽伏， ^1-7600

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7601]]

常以正法施化黔黎， ^1-7601

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7602]]

人民熾盛， ^1-7602

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7603]]

無有怨敵。 ^1-7603

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7604]]

國大夫人誕生三子，顏容端正，人所樂觀， ^1-7604

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7605]]

太子名曰摩訶波羅， ^1-7605

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7606]]

次子名曰摩訶提婆， ^1-7606

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7607]]

幼子名曰摩訶薩埵。 ^1-7607

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7608]]

是時大王為欲遊觀縱賞山林， ^1-7608

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7609]]

其三王子亦皆隨從， ^1-7609

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7610]]

為求花果，捨父周旋至大竹林，於中憩息。 ^1-7610

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7611]]

第一王子作如是言： ^1-7611

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7612]]

『我於今日，心甚驚惶， ^1-7612

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7613]]

於此林中將無猛獸損害於我？』 ^1-7613

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7614]]

第二王子復作是言： ^1-7614

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7615]]

『我於自身初無悋惜， ^1-7615

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7616]]

恐於所愛有別離苦。』 ^1-7616

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7617]]

第三王子白二兄曰： ^1-7617

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7618]]

『此是神仙所居處， ^1-7618

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7619]]

我無恐怖別離憂； ^1-7619

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7620]]

身心充遍生歡喜， ^1-7620

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7621]]

當獲殊勝諸功德。』 ^1-7621

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7622]]

時諸王子各說本心所念之事， ^1-7622

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7623]]

次復前行， ^1-7623

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7624]]

見有一虎產生七子， ^1-7624

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7625]]

纔經七日，諸子圍遶， ^1-7625

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7626]]

飢渴所逼， ^1-7626

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7627]]

身形羸瘦， ^1-7627

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7628]]

將死不久。 ^1-7628

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7629]]

第一王子作如是言：『哀哉！此虎產來七日， ^1-7629

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7630]]

七子圍遶，無暇求食， ^1-7630

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7631]]

飢渴所逼，必還噉子。』 ^1-7631

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7632]]

薩埵王子問言：『此虎每常所食何物？』 ^1-7632

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7633]]

第一王子答曰：「『虎豹豺師子，唯噉熱血肉； ^1-7633

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7634]]

更無餘飲食，可濟此虛羸。』 ^1-7634

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7635]]

第二王子聞此語已，作如是言： ^1-7635

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7636]]

『此虎羸瘦，飢渴所逼， ^1-7636

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7637]]

餘命無幾， ^1-7637

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7638]]

我等何能為求如是難得飲食？ ^1-7638

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7639]]

誰復為斯自捨身命， ^1-7639

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7640]]

濟其飢苦？』 ^1-7640

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7641]]

第一王子言： ^1-7641

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7642]]

『一切難捨無過己身。』 ^1-7642

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7643]]

薩埵王子言： ^1-7643

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7644]]

『我等今者於自己身各生愛戀， ^1-7644

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7645]]

復無智慧，不能於他而興利益， ^1-7645

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7646]]

然有上士懷大悲心， ^1-7646

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7647]]

常為利他亡身濟物。』 ^1-7647

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7648]]

復作是念：『我今此身於百千生， ^1-7648

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7649]]

虛棄爛壞， ^1-7649

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7650]]

曾無所益， ^1-7650

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7651]]

云何今日而不能捨以濟飢苦，如捐洟唾？』 ^1-7651

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7652]]

時諸王子作是議已， ^1-7652

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7653]]

各起慈心，悽傷愍念， ^1-7653

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7654]]

共觀羸虎，目不暫移， ^1-7654

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7655]]

徘徊久之，俱捨而去。 ^1-7655

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7656]]

爾時薩埵王子便作是念： ^1-7656

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7657]]

『我捨身命，今正是時。 ^1-7657

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7658]]

何以故？ ^1-7658

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7659]]

「『我從久來持此身， ^1-7659

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7660]]

臭穢膿流不可愛； ^1-7660

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7661]]

供給敷具并衣食， ^1-7661

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7662]]

象馬車乘及珍財。 ^1-7662

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7663]]

變壞之法體無常， ^1-7663

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7664]]

恒求難滿難保守； ^1-7664

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7665]]

雖常供養懷怨害，終歸棄我不知恩。 ^1-7665

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7666]]

「『復次，此身不堅， ^1-7666

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7667]]

於我無益， ^1-7667

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7668]]

可畏如賊， ^1-7668

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7669]]

不淨如糞， ^1-7669

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7670]]

我於今日，當使此身修廣大業， ^1-7670

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7671]]

於生死海作大舟航， ^1-7671

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7672]]

棄捨輪迴，令得出離。』 ^1-7672

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7673]]

復作是念：『若捨此身， ^1-7673

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7674]]

則捨無量癰疽惡疾，百千怖畏。 ^1-7674

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7675]]

是身唯有大小便利， ^1-7675

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7676]]

不堅如泡， ^1-7676

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7677]]

諸蟲所集， ^1-7677

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7678]]

血脈筋骨共相連持， ^1-7678

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7679]]

甚可厭患。 ^1-7679

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7680]]

是故我今應當棄捨， ^1-7680

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7681]]

以求無上究竟涅槃， ^1-7681

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7682]]

永離憂患無常苦惱， ^1-7682

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7683]]

生死休息， ^1-7683

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7684]]

斷諸塵累， ^1-7684

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7685]]

以定慧力圓滿熏修， ^1-7685

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7686]]

百福莊嚴 ^1-7686

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7687]]

成一切智， ^1-7687

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7688]]

諸佛所讚微妙法身， ^1-7688

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7689]]

既證得已， ^1-7689

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7690]]

施諸眾生無量法樂。』 ^1-7690

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7691]]

是時王子興大勇猛， ^1-7691

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7692]]

發弘誓願， ^1-7692

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7693]]

以大悲念增益其心， ^1-7693

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7694]]

慮彼二兄情懷怖懼，共為留難，不果所祈， ^1-7694

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7695]]

即便白言：『二兄前去，我且於後。』 ^1-7695

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7696]]

爾時王子摩訶薩埵還入林中， ^1-7696

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7697]]

至其虎所， ^1-7697

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7698]]

脫去衣服，置於竹上， ^1-7698

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7699]]

作是誓言： ^1-7699

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7700]]

「『我為法界諸眾生， ^1-7700

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7701]]

志求無上菩提處； ^1-7701

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7702]]

起大悲心不傾動， ^1-7702

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7703]]

當捨凡夫所愛身。 ^1-7703

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7704]]

菩提無患無熱惱， ^1-7704

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7705]]

諸有智者之所樂； ^1-7705

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7706]]

三界苦海諸眾生， ^1-7706

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7707]]

我今拔濟令安樂。』 ^1-7707

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7708]]

「是時王子作是言已， ^1-7708

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7709]]

於餓虎前委身而臥。 ^1-7709

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7710]]

由此菩薩慈悲威勢， ^1-7710

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7711]]

虎無能為。 ^1-7711

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7712]]

菩薩見已， ^1-7712

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7713]]

即上高山， ^1-7713

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7714]]

投身于地， ^1-7714

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7715]]

時諸神仙捧接王子，曾無傷損。 ^1-7715

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7716]]

復作是念：『虎今羸瘦，不能食我。』 ^1-7716

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7717]]

即起求刀，竟不能得， ^1-7717

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7718]]

即以乾竹刺頸出血， ^1-7718

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7719]]

漸近虎邊。 ^1-7719

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7720]]

是時大地六種震動，如風激水，涌沒不安， ^1-7720

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7721]]

日無精明，如羅睺障，諸方闇蔽，無復光輝， ^1-7721

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7722]]

天雨名華及妙香末，繽紛亂墜遍滿林中。 ^1-7722

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7723]]

爾時虛空有諸天眾，見是事已， ^1-7723

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7724]]

生隨喜心， ^1-7724

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7725]]

歎未曾有， ^1-7725

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7726]]

咸共讚言：『善哉大士！』 ^1-7726

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7727]]

即說頌曰： ^1-7727

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7728]]

『大士救護運悲心， ^1-7728

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7729]]

等視眾生如一子； ^1-7729

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7730]]

勇猛歡喜情無悋， ^1-7730

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7731]]

捨身濟苦福難思。 ^1-7731

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7732]]

定至真常勝妙處， ^1-7732

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7733]]

永離生死諸纏縛； ^1-7733

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7734]]

不久當獲菩提果， ^1-7734

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7735]]

寂靜安樂證無生。』 ^1-7735

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7736]]

「是時餓虎既見菩薩頸下血流， ^1-7736

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7737]]

即便舐血噉肉皆盡， ^1-7737

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7738]]

唯留餘骨。 ^1-7738

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7739]]

爾時第一王子見地動已， ^1-7739

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7740]]

告其弟曰： ^1-7740

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7741]]

「『大地山河皆震動， ^1-7741

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7742]]

諸方闇蔽日無光； ^1-7742

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7743]]

天花亂墜遍空中， ^1-7743

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7744]]

定是我弟捨身相。』 ^1-7744

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7745]]

「第二王子聞兄語已， ^1-7745

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7746]]

說伽他曰：「 ^1-7746

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7747]]

見彼餓虎身羸瘦； ^1-7747

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7748]]

飢苦所纏恐食子， ^1-7748

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7749]]

{『我聞薩埵慈悲語，} ^1-7749

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7750]]

我今疑弟捨其身。』 ^1-7750

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7751]]

「時二王子生大愁苦， ^1-7751

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7752]]

啼泣悲歎， ^1-7752

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7753]]

即共相隨還至虎所。 ^1-7753

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7754]]

見弟衣服在竹枝上， ^1-7754

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7755]]

骸骨及髮在處縱橫， ^1-7755

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7756]]

血流成泥霑污其地。 ^1-7756

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7757]]

見已，悶絕不能自持， ^1-7757

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7758]]

投身骨上， ^1-7758

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7759]]

久乃得蘇， ^1-7759

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7760]]

即起舉手哀號大哭。 ^1-7760

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7761]]

俱時歎曰： ^1-7761

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7762]]

『我弟貌端嚴， ^1-7762

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7763]]

父母偏愛念； ^1-7763

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7764]]

云何俱共出， ^1-7764

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7765]]

捨身而不歸？ ^1-7765

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7766]]

父母若問時， ^1-7766

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7767]]

我等如何答？ ^1-7767

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7768]]

寧可同捐命， ^1-7768

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7769]]

豈復自存身。』 ^1-7769

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7770]]

「時二王子悲泣懊惱， ^1-7770

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7771]]

漸捨而去， ^1-7771

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7772]]

時小王子所將侍從，互相謂曰： ^1-7772

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7773]]

『王子何在？ ^1-7773

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7774]]

宜共推求。』 ^1-7774

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7775]]

「爾時，國大夫人寢高樓上，便於夢中見不祥相， ^1-7775

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7776]]

被割兩乳， ^1-7776

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7777]]

牙齒墮落， ^1-7777

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7778]]

得三鴿鶵， ^1-7778

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7779]]

一為鷹奪， ^1-7779

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7780]]

二被驚怖。 ^1-7780

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7781]]

地動之時，夫人遂覺， ^1-7781

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7782]]

心大愁惱，作如是言： ^1-7782

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7783]]

「『何故今時大地動， ^1-7783

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7784]]

江河林樹皆搖震； ^1-7784

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7785]]

日無精光如覆蔽， ^1-7785

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7786]]

目𥌎乳動異常時。 ^1-7786

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7787]]

如箭射心憂苦逼， ^1-7787

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7788]]

遍身戰掉不安隱； ^1-7788

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7789]]

我之所夢不祥徵， ^1-7789

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7790]]

必有非常災變事。』 ^1-7790

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7791]]

「夫人兩乳忽然流出， ^1-7791

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7792]]

念此必有變怪之事。 ^1-7792

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7793]]

時有侍女聞外人言：『求覓王子，今猶未得。』 ^1-7793

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7794]]

心大驚怖， ^1-7794

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7795]]

即入宮中 ^1-7795

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7796]]

白夫人曰： ^1-7796

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7797]]

『大家知不？ ^1-7797

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7798]]

外聞諸人散覓王子，遍求不得。』 ^1-7798

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7799]]

時彼夫人聞是語已， ^1-7799

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7800]]

生大憂惱， ^1-7800

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7801]]

悲淚盈目， ^1-7801

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7802]]

至大王所，白言： ^1-7802

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7803]]

『大王！我聞外人作如是語：「失我最小所愛之子。」』 ^1-7803

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7804]]

王聞語已，驚惶失所， ^1-7804

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7805]]

悲哽而言：『苦哉！ ^1-7805

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7806]]

今日失我愛子。』 ^1-7806

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7807]]

即便抆淚慰喻夫人， ^1-7807

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7808]]

告言：『賢首！汝勿憂慼， ^1-7808

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7809]]

吾今共出求覓愛子。』 ^1-7809

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7810]]

王與大臣及諸人眾即共出城， ^1-7810

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7811]]

各各分散隨處求覓。 ^1-7811

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7812]]

未久之頃， ^1-7812

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7813]]

有一大臣前白王曰： ^1-7813

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7814]]

『聞王子在，願勿憂愁， ^1-7814

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7815]]

其最小者，今猶未見。』 ^1-7815

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7816]]

王聞是語，悲歎而言： ^1-7816

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7817]]

『苦哉！苦哉！失我愛子。 ^1-7817

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7818]]

「『初有子時歡喜少， ^1-7818

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7819]]

後失子時憂苦多； ^1-7819

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7820]]

若使我兒重壽命， ^1-7820

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7821]]

縱我身亡不為苦。』 ^1-7821

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7822]]

「夫人聞已，憂惱纏懷，如被箭中， ^1-7822

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7823]]

而嗟歎曰： ^1-7823

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7824]]

「『我之三子并侍從， ^1-7824

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7825]]

俱往林中共遊賞； ^1-7825

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7826]]

最小愛子獨不還， ^1-7826

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7827]]

定有乖離災厄事。』 ^1-7827

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7828]]

「次第二臣來至王所。 ^1-7828

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7829]]

王問臣曰：『愛子何在？』 ^1-7829

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7830]]

第二大臣懊惱啼泣， ^1-7830

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7831]]

喉舌乾燥，口不能言，竟無辭答。 ^1-7831

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7832]]

夫人問曰： ^1-7832

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7833]]

「『速報小子今何在？ ^1-7833

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7834]]

我身熱惱遍燒然； ^1-7834

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7835]]

悶亂荒迷失本心， ^1-7835

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7836]]

勿使我胸今破裂！』 ^1-7836

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7837]]

「時第二臣，即以王子捨身之事，具白王知。 ^1-7837

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7838]]

王及夫人聞其事已， ^1-7838

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7839]]

不勝悲噎，望捨身處，驟駕前行， ^1-7839

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7840]]

詣竹林所。至彼菩薩捨身之地， ^1-7840

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7841]]

見其骸骨隨處交橫， ^1-7841

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7842]]

俱時投地， ^1-7842

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7843]]

悶絕將死， ^1-7843

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7844]]

猶如猛風吹倒大樹， ^1-7844

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7845]]

心迷失緒，都無所知。 ^1-7845

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7846]]

時大臣等，以水遍灑 ^1-7846

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7847]]

王及夫人，良久乃蘇， ^1-7847

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7848]]

舉手而哭，諮嗟歎曰： ^1-7848

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7849]]

「『禍哉愛子端嚴相， ^1-7849

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7850]]

因何死苦先來逼？ ^1-7850

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7851]]

若我得在汝前亡， ^1-7851

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7852]]

豈見如斯大苦事。』 ^1-7852

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7853]]

「爾時夫人迷悶稍止， ^1-7853

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7854]]

頭髮蓬亂， ^1-7854

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7855]]

兩手椎胸， ^1-7855

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7856]]

宛轉于地，如魚處陸， ^1-7856

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7857]]

若牛失子，悲泣而言： ^1-7857

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7858]]

「『我子誰屠割， ^1-7858

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7859]]

餘骨散于地？ ^1-7859

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7860]]

失我所愛子， ^1-7860

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7861]]

憂悲不自勝。 ^1-7861

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7862]]

苦哉誰殺子， ^1-7862

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7863]]

致斯憂惱事； ^1-7863

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7864]]

我心非金剛， ^1-7864

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7865]]

云何而不破？ ^1-7865

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7866]]

我夢中所見，兩乳皆被割； ^1-7866

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7867]]

牙齒悉墮落， ^1-7867

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7868]]

今遭大苦痛。 ^1-7868

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7869]]

又夢三鴿鶵， ^1-7869

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7870]]

一被鷹擒去； ^1-7870

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7871]]

今失所愛子， ^1-7871

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7872]]

惡相表非虛。』 ^1-7872

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7873]]

「爾時，大王及於夫人并二王子，盡哀號哭， ^1-7873

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7874]]

瓔珞不御， ^1-7874

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7875]]

與諸人眾共收菩薩遺身舍利， ^1-7875

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7876]]

為於供養，置窣堵波中。 ^1-7876

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7877]]

阿難陀！汝等應知！此即是彼菩薩舍利。」 ^1-7877

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7878]]

復告阿難陀： ^1-7878

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7879]]

「我於昔時，雖具煩惱貪瞋癡等， ^1-7879

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7880]]

能於地獄、餓鬼、傍生五趣之中， ^1-7880

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7881]]

隨緣救濟， ^1-7881

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7882]]

令得出離。 ^1-7882

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7883]]

何況今時煩惱都盡， ^1-7883

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7884]]

無復餘習， ^1-7884

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7885]]

號天人師， ^1-7885

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7886]]

具一切智， ^1-7886

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7887]]

而不能為一一眾生，經於多劫 ^1-7887

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7888]]

在地獄中及於餘處， ^1-7888

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7889]]

代受眾苦， ^1-7889

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7890]]

令出生死煩惱輪迴！」 ^1-7890

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7891]]

爾時世尊，欲重宣此義而說頌曰： ^1-7891

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7892]]

「我念過去世， ^1-7892

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7893]]

無量無數劫； ^1-7893

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7894]]

或時作國王， ^1-7894

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7895]]

或復為王子。 ^1-7895

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7896]]

常行於大施， ^1-7896

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7897]]

及捨所愛身； ^1-7897

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7898]]

願出離生死，至妙菩提處。 ^1-7898

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7899]]

昔時有大國， ^1-7899

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7900]]

國主名大車； ^1-7900

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7901]]

王子名勇猛， ^1-7901

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7902]]

常施心無悋。 ^1-7902

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7903]]

王子有二兄， ^1-7903

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7904]]

號大渠大天； ^1-7904

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7905]]

三人同出遊， ^1-7905

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7906]]

漸至山林所。 ^1-7906

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7907]]

見虎飢所逼， ^1-7907

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7908]]

便生如是心： ^1-7908

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7909]]

『此虎飢火燒， ^1-7909

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7910]]

更無餘可食。』 ^1-7910

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7911]]

大士覩如斯， ^1-7911

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7912]]

恐其將食子； ^1-7912

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7913]]

捨身無所顧， ^1-7913

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7914]]

救子不令傷。 ^1-7914

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7915]]

大地及諸山， ^1-7915

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7916]]

一時皆震動； ^1-7916

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7917]]

江海皆騰躍， ^1-7917

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7918]]

驚波水逆流。 ^1-7918

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7919]]

天地失光明， ^1-7919

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7920]]

昬冥無所見； ^1-7920

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7921]]

林野諸禽獸， ^1-7921

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7922]]

飛奔喪所依。 ^1-7922

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7923]]

二兄怪不還， ^1-7923

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7924]]

憂慼生悲苦； ^1-7924

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7925]]

即與諸侍從， ^1-7925

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7926]]

林藪遍尋求。 ^1-7926

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7927]]

兄弟共籌議， ^1-7927

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7928]]

復往深山處； ^1-7928

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7929]]

四顧無所有， ^1-7929

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7930]]

見虎處空林。 ^1-7930

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7931]]

其母并七子， ^1-7931

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7932]]

口皆有血污； ^1-7932

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7933]]

殘骨并餘髮， ^1-7933

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7934]]

縱橫在地上。 ^1-7934

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7935]]

復見有流血， ^1-7935

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7936]]

散在樹林所； ^1-7936

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7937]]

二兄既見已， ^1-7937

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7938]]

心生大恐怖。 ^1-7938

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7939]]

悶絕俱躄地， ^1-7939

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7940]]

荒迷不覺知； ^1-7940

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7941]]

塵土坌其身， ^1-7941

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7942]]

六情皆失念。 ^1-7942

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7943]]

王子諸侍從， ^1-7943

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7944]]

啼泣心憂惱； ^1-7944

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7945]]

以水灑令蘇， ^1-7945

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7946]]

舉手號咷哭。 ^1-7946

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7947]]

菩薩捨身時， ^1-7947

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7948]]

慈母在宮內； ^1-7948

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7949]]

五百諸婇女， ^1-7949

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7950]]

共受於妙樂。 ^1-7950

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7951]]

夫人之兩乳， ^1-7951

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7952]]

忽然自流出； ^1-7952

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7953]]

遍體如針刺， ^1-7953

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7954]]

苦痛不能安。 ^1-7954

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7955]]

欻生失子想， ^1-7955

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7956]]

憂箭苦傷心； ^1-7956

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7957]]

即白大王知， ^1-7957

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7958]]

陳斯苦惱事。 ^1-7958

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7959]]

悲泣不堪忍， ^1-7959

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7960]]

哀聲向王說： ^1-7960

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7961]]

『大王今當知， ^1-7961

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7962]]

我生大苦惱。 ^1-7962

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7963]]

兩乳忽流出， ^1-7963

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7964]]

禁止不隨心； ^1-7964

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7965]]

如針遍刺身， ^1-7965

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7966]]

煩宛胸欲破。 ^1-7966

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7967]]

我先夢惡徵， ^1-7967

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7968]]

必當失愛子； ^1-7968

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7969]]

願王濟我命， ^1-7969

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7970]]

知兒存與亡。 ^1-7970

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7971]]

夢見三鴿鶵， ^1-7971

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7972]]

小者是愛子； ^1-7972

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7973]]

忽被鷹奪去， ^1-7973

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7974]]

悲愁難具陳。 ^1-7974

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7975]]

我今沒憂海， ^1-7975

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7976]]

趣死將不久； ^1-7976

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7977]]

恐子命不全， ^1-7977

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7978]]

願為速求覓。 ^1-7978

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7979]]

又聞外人語，小子求不得； ^1-7979

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7980]]

我今意不安， ^1-7980

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7981]]

願王哀愍我。』 ^1-7981

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7982]]

夫人白王已， ^1-7982

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7983]]

舉身而躄地； ^1-7983

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7984]]

悲痛心悶絕， ^1-7984

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7985]]

荒迷不覺知。 ^1-7985

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7986]]

婇女見夫人，悶絕在於地； ^1-7986

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7987]]

舉聲皆大哭， ^1-7987

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7988]]

憂惶失所依。 ^1-7988

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7989]]

王聞如是語， ^1-7989

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7990]]

懷憂不自勝； ^1-7990

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7991]]

因命諸群臣， ^1-7991

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7992]]

尋求所愛子。 ^1-7992

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7993]]

皆共出城外， ^1-7993

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7994]]

各隨處追覓； ^1-7994

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7995]]

涕泣問諸人：『王子今何在？ ^1-7995

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7996]]

今者為存亡， ^1-7996

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7997]]

誰知所去處？ ^1-7997

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7998]]

云何令我見， ^1-7998

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-7999]]

解我憂悲心？』 ^1-7999

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8000]]

諸人悉共傳，咸言王子死； ^1-8000

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8001]]

聞者皆傷悼， ^1-8001

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8002]]

悲歎苦難裁。 ^1-8002

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8003]]

爾時大車王， ^1-8003

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8004]]

悲號從座起； ^1-8004

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8005]]

即就夫人處， ^1-8005

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8006]]

以水灑其身。 ^1-8006

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8007]]

夫人蒙水灑， ^1-8007

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8008]]

久乃得醒悟； ^1-8008

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8009]]

悲啼以問王： ^1-8009

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8010]]

『我兒今在不？』 ^1-8010

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8011]]

王告夫人曰： ^1-8011

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8012]]

『我已使諸人， ^1-8012

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8013]]

四向求王子， ^1-8013

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8014]]

尚未有消息。』 ^1-8014

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8015]]

王又告夫人： ^1-8015

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8016]]

『汝莫生煩惱， ^1-8016

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8017]]

且當自安慰， ^1-8017

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8018]]

可共出追尋。』 ^1-8018

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8019]]

王即與夫人， ^1-8019

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8020]]

嚴駕而前進； ^1-8020

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8021]]

號動聲悽感， ^1-8021

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8022]]

憂心若火然。 ^1-8022

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8023]]

士庶百千萬， ^1-8023

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8024]]

亦隨王出城； ^1-8024

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8025]]

各欲求王子，悲號聲不絕。 ^1-8025

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8026]]

王求愛子故， ^1-8026

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8027]]

目視於四方； ^1-8027

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8028]]

被髮身塗血， ^1-8028

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8029]]

遍體蒙塵土， ^1-8029

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8030]]

悲哭逆前來。 ^1-8030

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8031]]

{見有一人來，} ^1-8031

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8032]]

王見是惡相， ^1-8032

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8033]]

倍復生憂惱， ^1-8033

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8034]]

王便舉兩手， ^1-8034

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8035]]

哀號不自裁。 ^1-8035

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8036]]

初有一大臣，忩忙至王所， ^1-8036

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8037]]

進白大王曰： ^1-8037

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8038]]

『幸願勿悲哀； ^1-8038

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8039]]

王之所愛子， ^1-8039

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8040]]

今雖求未獲。 ^1-8040

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8041]]

不久當來至， ^1-8041

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8042]]

以釋大王憂。』 ^1-8042

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8043]]

王復更前行， ^1-8043

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8044]]

見次大臣至， ^1-8044

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8045]]

其臣詣王所， ^1-8045

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8046]]

流淚白王言： ^1-8046

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8047]]

『二子今現在， ^1-8047

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8048]]

被憂火所逼； ^1-8048

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8049]]

其第三王子， ^1-8049

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8050]]

已被無常吞。 ^1-8050

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8051]]

見餓虎初生， ^1-8051

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8052]]

將欲食其子； ^1-8052

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8053]]

彼薩埵王子，見此起悲心， ^1-8053

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8054]]

願求無上道， ^1-8054

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8055]]

當度一切眾。 ^1-8055

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8056]]

繫想妙菩提， ^1-8056

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8057]]

廣大深如海； ^1-8057

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8058]]

即上高山頂， ^1-8058

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8059]]

投身餓虎前。 ^1-8059

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8060]]

虎羸不能食， ^1-8060

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8061]]

以竹自傷頸； ^1-8061

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8062]]

遂噉王子身， ^1-8062

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8063]]

唯有餘骸骨。』 ^1-8063

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8064]]

時王及夫人， ^1-8064

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8065]]

聞已俱悶絕； ^1-8065

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8066]]

心沒於憂海， ^1-8066

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8067]]

煩惱火燒然。 ^1-8067

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8068]]

臣以栴檀水， ^1-8068

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8069]]

灑王及夫人； ^1-8069

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8070]]

俱起大悲號， ^1-8070

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8071]]

舉手椎胸臆。 ^1-8071

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8072]]

第三大臣來， ^1-8072

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8073]]

白王如是語： ^1-8073

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8074]]

『我見二王子， ^1-8074

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8075]]

悶絕在林中。 ^1-8075

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8076]]

臣以冷水灑， ^1-8076

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8077]]

爾乃暫蘇息； ^1-8077

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8078]]

顧視於四方， ^1-8078

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8079]]

如猛火周遍。 ^1-8079

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8080]]

暫起而還伏， ^1-8080

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8081]]

悲號不自勝； ^1-8081

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8082]]

舉手以哀言， ^1-8082

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8083]]

稱歎弟希有。』 ^1-8083

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8084]]

王聞如是說， ^1-8084

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8085]]

倍增憂火煎； ^1-8085

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8086]]

夫人大號咷， ^1-8086

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8087]]

高聲作是語： ^1-8087

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8088]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8361]]

 ^1-8361

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8362]]

我今隨力稱讚如來少分功德， ^1-8362

爾時， ^1-8367

具大辯才， ^1-8370

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8371]]

相好圓明， ^1-8374

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8375]]

普利一切。 ^1-8375

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8376]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8377]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8378]]

{}爾時， 世尊普告無量菩薩，及諸人天一切大眾：「 ^1-8378

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8416]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8420]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8421]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8455]]

說伽他曰：_「 ^1-8465

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8466]]

{} 正義相應經；_ ^1-8466

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8467]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8471]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8475]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8476]]

若有持此經， ^1-8476

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8477]]

能伏諸煩惱；_ ^1-8477

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8478]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8479]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8484]]

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8485]]

諸佛妙菩提， ^1-8485

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8486]]

於此經中說；_ ^1-8486

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8487]]

若持此經者， ^1-8487

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8488]]

是供養如來。 ^1-8488

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8489]]

_我當持此經， ^1-8489

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8490]]

為俱胝天說；_ ^1-8490

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8491]]

恭敬聽聞者， ^1-8491

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8492]]

勸至菩提處。 」_ ^1-8492

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8493]]

爾時，慈氏菩薩合掌恭敬， ^1-8493

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8494]]

說伽他曰：_「 ^1-8494

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8495]]

若見住菩提， ^1-8495

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8496]]

與為不請友；_ ^1-8496

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8497]]

乃至捨身命， ^1-8497

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8498]]

為護此經王。 ^1-8498

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8499]]

_我聞如是法， ^1-8499

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8500]]

當往覩史天；_ ^1-8500

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8501]]

由世尊加護， ^1-8501

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8502]]

廣為人天說。 」_ ^1-8502

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8503]]

爾時，上座大迦葉波合掌恭敬， ^1-8503

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8504]]

說伽他曰：_「 ^1-8504

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8505]]

佛於聲聞乘， ^1-8505

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8506]]

說我鮮智慧；_ ^1-8506

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8507]]

我今隨自力， ^1-8507

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8508]]

護持如是經。 ^1-8508

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8509]]

_若有持此經， ^1-8509

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8510]]

我當攝受彼；_ ^1-8510

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8511]]

授其詞辯力， ^1-8511

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8512]]

常隨讚善哉。 」_ ^1-8512

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8513]]

爾時，具壽阿難陀合掌向佛， ^1-8513

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8514]]

說伽他曰：_「 ^1-8514

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8515]]

我親從佛聞， ^1-8515

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8516]]

無量眾經典；_ ^1-8516

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8517]]

{}{深妙法中王。} ^1-8517

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8518]]

{未曾聞如是，}{} ^1-8518

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8519]]

_我今聞是經， 親於佛前受；_ ^1-8519

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8520]]

諸樂菩提者， ^1-8520

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8521]]

當為廣宣通。 」_ ^1-8521

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8522]]

爾時，世尊見諸菩薩人天大眾各各發心，於此經典流通擁護，勸進菩薩廣利眾生， ^1-8522

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8523]]

讚言：「 善哉！善哉！ ^1-8523

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8524]]

汝等能於如是微妙經王虔誠流布， ^1-8524

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8525]]

乃至於我般涅槃後， ^1-8525

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8526]]

不令散滅，即是無上菩提正因， ^1-8526

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8527]]

所獲功德於恒沙劫說不能盡。 ^1-8527

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8528]]

若有苾芻、苾芻尼、鄔波索迦、鄔波斯迦，及餘善男子、善女人等， 供養恭敬， ^1-8528

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8529]]

書寫流通， ^1-8529

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8530]]

為人解說，所獲功德亦復如是， ^1-8530

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8531]]

是故汝等應勤修習。 」_ ^1-8531

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8532]]

爾時，無量無邊恒沙大眾，聞佛說已， ^1-8532

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8533]]

皆大歡喜， ^1-8533

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8534]]

信受奉行。 ^1-8534

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8535]]

{金光明最勝王經} ^1-8535

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8536]]

{付囑品第三十一} ^1-8536

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8537]]

_金光明最勝王經卷第十 ^1-8537

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8538]]

 ^1-8538

![[1-SOURCES/Text/bo-toh555-the-stra-of-the-sublime-golden-light.md#^1-8539]]

 ^1-8539
